===== BEGIN SOURCE test/test-widget-state-runtime.js | original-bytes=3795 | sha256=ee38984d3bf0295b3ed449e935e9699761a12d9b7e8c4f09272a5e6e94611998 =====
import assert from 'assert';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'url';

import { createWidgetStateStorage } from '../dist/ui/shared/widget-state.js';

function createMockSessionStorage() {
  const data = new Map();
  return {
    data,
    getItem(key) {
      return data.has(key) ? data.get(key) : null;
    },
    setItem(key, value) {
      data.set(key, value);
    },
    removeItem(key) {
      data.delete(key);
    },
  };
}

function createMockWindow(pathname, sessionStorage, name = '') {
  return {
    location: { pathname },
    sessionStorage,
    name,
  };
}

async function testWidgetStateUsesPerFrameKeys() {
  console.log('\n--- Test: widget state keeps same-origin iframes isolated ---');

  const originalWindow = globalThis.window;
  const sessionStorage = createMockSessionStorage();

  try {
    const firstWindow = createMockWindow('/ui/file-preview/index.html', sessionStorage);
    globalThis.window = firstWindow;
    const firstStorage = createWidgetStateStorage((value) => typeof value === 'string');
    firstStorage.write('first payload');

    assert.ok(firstWindow.name.includes('__mcp_device_widget_id__:'), 'The first frame should persist its widget id in window.name');
    assert.strictEqual(firstStorage.read(), 'first payload', 'The first frame should read back its own cached payload');

    const secondWindow = createMockWindow('/ui/file-preview/index.html', sessionStorage);
    globalThis.window = secondWindow;
    const secondStorage = createWidgetStateStorage((value) => typeof value === 'string');
    secondStorage.write('second payload');

    assert.ok(secondWindow.name.includes('__mcp_device_widget_id__:'), 'The second frame should persist its widget id in window.name');
    assert.notStrictEqual(secondWindow.name, firstWindow.name, 'Visible frames should get distinct widget ids');
    assert.strictEqual(secondStorage.read(), 'second payload', 'The second frame should read back its own cached payload');
    assert.strictEqual(sessionStorage.data.size, 2, 'Two same-origin frames should write to separate cache keys');

    const refreshedFirstWindow = createMockWindow('/ui/file-preview/index.html', sessionStorage, firstWindow.name);
    globalThis.window = refreshedFirstWindow;
    const refreshedFirstStorage = createWidgetStateStorage((value) => typeof value === 'string');
    assert.strictEqual(refreshedFirstStorage.read(), 'first payload', 'Reloading the first frame should preserve its cache slot');

    const refreshedSecondWindow = createMockWindow('/ui/file-preview/index.html', sessionStorage, secondWindow.name);
    globalThis.window = refreshedSecondWindow;
    const refreshedSecondStorage = createWidgetStateStorage((value) => typeof value === 'string');
    assert.strictEqual(refreshedSecondStorage.read(), 'second payload', 'Reloading the second frame should preserve its own cache slot');
  } finally {
    globalThis.window = originalWindow;
  }

  console.log('✓ widget state keeps same-origin iframes isolated across refresh');
}

export default async function runTests() {
  try {
    await testWidgetStateUsesPerFrameKeys();
    console.log('\n✅ Widget state runtime tests passed!');
    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
    console.error('❌ Test failed:', message);
    if (error instanceof Error && error.stack) {
      console.error(error.stack);
    }
    return false;
  }
}

if (process.argv[1] && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href) {
  runTests().then((success) => {
    process.exit(success ? 0 : 1);
  }).catch((error) => {
    console.error('❌ Unhandled error:', error);
    process.exit(1);
  });
}

===== BEGIN SOURCE test/test-windows-device-service.js | original-bytes=9305 | sha256=74e35404f01aca0fda8a8e5dc27a0d2a2a80f42010a31c168aa8586f974094e4 =====
﻿import assert from 'assert';
import fs from 'fs/promises';
import os from 'os';
import path from 'path';

import {
  WindowsDeviceService,
  buildWindowsTaskName,
  buildWindowsRunnerScript
} from '../dist/device/windows-service.js';

function testDeterministicTaskNameAndRunnerAreSecretFree() {
  assert.equal(buildWindowsTaskName('device_1'), 'MCP-Device-device_1');
  const script = buildWindowsRunnerScript({
    gatewayUrl: 'https://mcp.example.test',
    nodePath: 'C:\\Program Files\\nodejs\\node.exe',
    entrypoint: 'C:\\Program Files\\mcp-device\\dist\\index.js',
    runtimeDir: 'C:\\Users\\test\\.mcp-device\\runtime'
  });
  assert(script.includes("Set-Location -LiteralPath 'C:\\Users\\test\\.mcp-device\\runtime'"));
  assert(!script.includes('mcp.example.test'));
  assert(!script.includes('MCP_GATEWAY_URL'));
  assert(!script.includes('MCP_GATEWAY_ALLOWED_ROOTS'));
  assert(script.includes(' --service'));
  assert(!script.includes(' remote'));
  assert(!/enrollment|pairing_grant|private_key|bearer/i.test(script));
}

async function testLifecycleUsesOneTaskAndRemovesRunner() {
  const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-device-service-'));
  const calls = [];
  const execFile = async (file, args) => {
    calls.push({ file, args });
    if (file.toLowerCase().includes('powershell') && args.join(' ').includes('Get-ScheduledTask')) {
      return { stdout: 'Ready\n', stderr: '' };
    }
    return { stdout: '', stderr: '' };
  };
  const service = new WindowsDeviceService({
    platform: 'win32',
    execFile,
    runnerPath: path.join(root, 'run-device.ps1'),
    nodePath: 'C:\\node.exe',
    entrypoint: 'C:\\dc\\dist\\index.js',
    runtimeDir: path.join(root, 'runtime')
  });
  await service.install({ deviceId: 'device-test', gatewayUrl: 'https://gateway.example.test' });
  const runner = await fs.readFile(path.join(root, 'run-device.ps1'), 'utf8');
  assert(!runner.includes('https://gateway.example.test'));
  assert(calls.some(call => call.file.toLowerCase().includes('schtasks') && call.args.includes('/Create')));
  assert(calls.some(call => call.args.includes('MCP-Device-device-test')));
  await service.start('device-test');
  await service.stop('device-test');
  const status = await service.status('device-test');
  assert.deepEqual(status, { installed: true, running: false, autostart: true, registration: 'task', taskName: 'MCP-Device-device-test' });
  await service.uninstall('device-test');
  await assert.rejects(fs.readFile(path.join(root, 'run-device.ps1'), 'utf8'), /ENOENT/);
  assert(calls.some(call => call.file.toLowerCase().includes('schtasks') && call.args.includes('/Delete')));
  await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
}

async function testSchTasksAccessDeniedFallsBackToUserRunKey() {
  const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-device-service-fallback-'));
  const calls = [];
  let processChecks = 0;
  const execFile = async (file, args) => {
    calls.push({ file, args });
    const joined = args.join(' ');
    if (file.toLowerCase().includes('schtasks') && args.includes('/Create')) {
      const error = new Error('Command failed');
      error.stderr = 'ERROR: Access is denied.';
      throw error;
    }
    if (file.toLowerCase().includes('schtasks')) {
      const error = new Error('Command failed');
      error.stderr = 'ERROR: The system cannot find the file specified.';
      throw error;
    }
    if (file.toLowerCase().includes('powershell') && joined.includes('Get-ScheduledTask')) {
      throw new Error('task missing');
    }
    if (file.toLowerCase().includes('reg.exe') && args[0] === 'query') {
      return { stdout: 'MCP-Device-device-fallback    REG_SZ    powershell.exe', stderr: '' };
    }
    if (file.toLowerCase().includes('powershell') && joined.includes('Get-CimInstance Win32_Process') && joined.includes("'Running'")) {
      processChecks += 1;
      return { stdout: processChecks === 1 ? 'Stopped\n' : 'Running\n', stderr: '' };
    }
    return { stdout: '', stderr: '' };
  };
  const service = new WindowsDeviceService({
    platform: 'win32',
    execFile,
    runnerPath: path.join(root, 'run-device.ps1'),
    nodePath: 'C:\\node.exe',
    entrypoint: 'C:\\dc\\dist\\index.js',
    runtimeDir: path.join(root, 'runtime')
  });

  await service.install({ deviceId: 'device-fallback', gatewayUrl: 'https://gateway.example.test' });
  assert(calls.some(call => call.file.toLowerCase().includes('reg.exe') && call.args[0] === 'add'));
  await service.start('device-fallback');
  assert(calls.some(call => call.file.toLowerCase().includes('powershell') && call.args.join(' ').includes('Start-Process')));
  const status = await service.status('device-fallback');
  assert.deepEqual(status, { installed: true, running: true, autostart: true, registration: 'run', taskName: 'MCP-Device-device-fallback' });
  await assert.rejects(service.stop('device-fallback'), /refusing PID-only termination/i);
  assert.equal(calls.some(call => call.args.join(' ').includes('taskkill.exe')), false);
  await service.uninstall('device-fallback');
  assert(calls.some(call => call.file.toLowerCase().includes('reg.exe') && call.args[0] === 'delete'));
  await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
}

async function testManualModeKeepsRunnerAndStartsDirectly() {
  const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-device-service-manual-'));
  const calls = [];
  const runnerPath = path.join(root, 'run-device.ps1');
  let taskExists = false;
  let runEntryExists = false;
  let running = false;
  const execFile = async (file, args) => {
    calls.push({ file, args });
    const lower = file.toLowerCase();
    const joined = args.join(' ');
    if (lower.includes('schtasks') && args.includes('/Create')) { taskExists = true; return { stdout: '', stderr: '' }; }
    if (lower.includes('schtasks') && args.includes('/Delete')) { taskExists = false; return { stdout: '', stderr: '' }; }
    if (lower.includes('schtasks') && args.includes('/Run')) {
      if (!taskExists) { const error = new Error('missing'); error.stderr = 'does not exist'; throw error; }
      running = true; return { stdout: '', stderr: '' };
    }
    if (lower.includes('schtasks') && args.includes('/End')) { running = false; return { stdout: '', stderr: '' }; }
    if (lower.includes('reg.exe') && args[0] === 'query') {
      if (!runEntryExists) throw new Error('missing');
      return { stdout: 'entry', stderr: '' };
    }
    if (lower.includes('reg.exe') && args[0] === 'add') { runEntryExists = true; return { stdout: '', stderr: '' }; }
    if (lower.includes('reg.exe') && args[0] === 'delete') { runEntryExists = false; return { stdout: '', stderr: '' }; }
    if (lower.includes('powershell') && joined.includes('Get-ScheduledTask')) {
      if (!taskExists) throw new Error('task missing');
      return { stdout: running ? 'Running\n' : 'Ready\n', stderr: '' };
    }
    if (lower.includes('powershell') && joined.includes("'Running'")) return { stdout: running ? 'Running\n' : 'Stopped\n', stderr: '' };
    if (lower.includes('powershell') && joined.includes('Start-Process')) { running = true; return { stdout: '', stderr: '' }; }
    if (lower.includes('powershell') && joined.includes('taskkill.exe')) { running = false; return { stdout: '', stderr: '' }; }
    return { stdout: '', stderr: '' };
  };
  const service = new WindowsDeviceService({
    platform: 'win32',
    execFile,
    runnerPath,
    nodePath: 'C:\\node.exe',
    entrypoint: 'C:\\dc\\dist\\index.js',
    runtimeDir: path.join(root, 'runtime')
  });
  await service.install({ deviceId: 'device-manual', gatewayUrl: 'https://gateway.example.test' });
  await service.setAutostart('device-manual', false);
  let status = await service.status('device-manual');
  assert.deepEqual(status, { installed: true, running: false, autostart: false, registration: 'manual', taskName: 'MCP-Device-device-manual' });
  await service.start('device-manual');
  assert(calls.some(call => call.file.toLowerCase().includes('powershell') && call.args.join(' ').includes('Start-Process')));
  status = await service.status('device-manual');
  assert.equal(status.running, true);
  await service.setAutostart('device-manual', true);
  status = await service.status('device-manual');
  assert.equal(status.autostart, true);
  await service.setAutostart('device-manual', false);
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(runnerPath, 'utf8').then(() => true), true);
  await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
}

async function testNonWindowsRefusesLifecycle() {
  const service = new WindowsDeviceService({ platform: 'linux', execFile: async () => ({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }) });
  await assert.rejects(service.install({ deviceId: 'device-test', gatewayUrl: 'https://gateway.example.test' }), /Windows/i);
}

===== BEGIN SOURCE test/test-windows-local-storage.js | original-bytes=3818 | sha256=a3e48bf477aad3734eb4f2ddcde9a632670d98f75ff25f11bb1a5a80f8ea48d1 =====
import assert from 'assert';
import fs from 'fs/promises';
import { generateKeyPairSync } from 'crypto';
import os from 'os';
import path from 'path';

import { GatewayDeviceConfigStore } from '../dist/device/gateway-config.js';
import { GatewayDeviceIdentity } from '../dist/device/gateway-identity.js';

const forbiddenSecretOptions = {
  platform: 'win32',
  protectSecret: async () => { throw new Error('legacy secret API must not run'); },
  unprotectSecret: async () => { throw new Error('legacy secret API must not run'); }
};

async function testWindowsIdentityPersistsWithoutSecretApi() {
  const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-device-plain-identity-'));
  const identityPath = path.join(root, 'identity.json');
  const pair = generateKeyPairSync('ed25519');
  const seeded = {
    deviceId: 'plain-device',
    publicKeyPem: pair.publicKey.export({ type: 'spki', format: 'pem' }).toString(),
    privateKeyPem: pair.privateKey.export({ type: 'pkcs8', format: 'pem' }).toString(),
    enrolled: true
  };
  await fs.writeFile(identityPath, JSON.stringify(seeded, null, 2));
  try {
    const identity = new GatewayDeviceIdentity(identityPath, forbiddenSecretOptions);
    const loaded = await identity.loadOrCreate();
    assert.equal(loaded.deviceId, seeded.deviceId);
    assert.equal(loaded.privateKeyPem, seeded.privateKeyPem);
    const persisted = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(identityPath, 'utf8'));
    assert.equal(persisted.privateKeyPem, seeded.privateKeyPem);
    assert.equal(persisted.enrolled, true);
  } finally {
    await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

async function testLegacyProtectedIdentityIsArchivedWithoutDecrypting() {
  const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-device-legacy-identity-'));
  const identityPath = path.join(root, 'identity.json');
  const legacy = {
    version: 2,
    deviceId: 'legacy-protected-device',
    publicKeyPem: 'legacy-public-key',
    privateKey: { scheme: 'dpapi-current-user-v1', blob: 'opaque-do-not-decrypt' },
    enrolled: true
  };
  await fs.writeFile(identityPath, JSON.stringify(legacy, null, 2));
  try {
    const identity = new GatewayDeviceIdentity(identityPath, forbiddenSecretOptions);
    const loaded = await identity.loadOrCreate();
    assert.notEqual(loaded.deviceId, legacy.deviceId);
    assert.equal(loaded.enrolled, false);
    const files = await fs.readdir(root);
    assert(files.some(name => name.startsWith('identity.json.legacy-protected.')));
    const persisted = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(identityPath, 'utf8'));
    assert.equal(typeof persisted.privateKeyPem, 'string');
    assert(!('privateKey' in persisted));
  } finally {
    await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

async function testWindowsProxyPersistsWithoutSecretApi() {
  const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-device-plain-proxy-'));
  const configPath = path.join(root, 'gateway-config.json');
  const proxyUrl = 'http://proxy-user:proxy-pass@127.0.0.1:8080';
  try {
    const store = new GatewayDeviceConfigStore(configPath, forbiddenSecretOptions);
    await store.update({ proxy: { mode: 'configured', url: proxyUrl } });
    const raw = await fs.readFile(configPath, 'utf8');
    assert(raw.includes('proxy-user'));
    assert(raw.includes('proxy-pass'));
    assert.deepEqual((await store.load()).proxy, { mode: 'configured', url: `${proxyUrl}/` });
  } finally {
    await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

await testWindowsIdentityPersistsWithoutSecretApi();
await testLegacyProtectedIdentityIsArchivedWithoutDecrypting();
await testWindowsProxyPersistsWithoutSecretApi();
console.log('Windows local storage avoids legacy secret APIs');

import path from 'path';
import fs from 'fs/promises';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
import { handleEditBlock } from '../dist/handlers/edit-search-handlers.js';
import { configManager } from '../dist/config-manager.js';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const TEST_FILEPATH = path.join(__dirname, 'test.txt')

async function setup() {
  // Save original config to restore later
  const originalConfig = await configManager.getConfig();
  return originalConfig;
}


/**
 * Teardown function to clean up after tests
 */
async function teardown(originalConfig) {

  // Reset configuration to original
  await configManager.updateConfig(originalConfig);

  await fs.rm(TEST_FILEPATH, { force: true, recursive: true });
  // Clean up test directories
  console.log('✓ Teardown: test directories cleaned up and config restored');
}



// Export the main test function
async function testEditBlock() {
    try {
        await configManager.setValue('allowedDirectories', [__dirname]);
        
        // Create a test file
        const fs = await import('fs/promises');
        await fs.writeFile(TEST_FILEPATH, 'This is old content to replace');

        // Test handleEditBlock
        const result = await handleEditBlock({
            file_path: TEST_FILEPATH,
            old_string: 'old content',
            new_string: 'new content',
            expected_replacements: 1
        });

        console.log('Edit block result:', result);

        const fileContent = await fs.readFile(TEST_FILEPATH, 'utf8');
        console.log('File content after replacement:', fileContent);

        if (fileContent.includes('new content')) {
            console.log('Replace test passed!');
        } else {
            throw new Error('Replace test failed!');
        }

        // Cleanup
        await fs.unlink(TEST_FILEPATH);
        console.log('All tests passed! 🎉');
        return true;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Test failed:', error);
        return false;
    }
}


// Export the main test function
export default async function runTests() {
    let originalConfig;
    try {
      originalConfig = await setup();
      await testEditBlock();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('❌ Test failed:', error.message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      if (originalConfig) {
        await teardown(originalConfig);
      }
    }
    return true;
}


// If this file is run directly (not imported), execute the test
if (import.meta.url === `file://${process.argv[1]}`) {
runTests().catch(error => {
    console.error('❌ Unhandled error:', error);
    process.exit(1);
});
}

// Test script to verify enhanced file reading
import { readFileInternal } from '../dist/tools/filesystem.js';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
import { dirname, join } from 'path';

// Get the test directory path
const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = dirname(__filename);
const TEST_FILE_PATH = join(__dirname, 'test_output', 'file_with_1500_lines.txt');

async function testEnhancedReading() {
    let testsPassed = 0;
    let totalTests = 3;
    
    console.log('Testing enhanced file reading with our 1500-line file...');
    
    try {
        // Test 1: Read first 10 lines
        console.log('\n=== Test 1: Read first 10 lines ===');
        try {
            const result1 = await readFileInternal(TEST_FILE_PATH, 0, 10);
            console.log('Result length:', result1.length);
            console.log('First few lines of result:');
            console.log(result1.substring(0, 200) + '...');
            
            // Validate the result
            const lines = result1.split('\n').filter(line => line.length > 0);
            if (lines.length === 10 && lines[0].includes('line 1')) {
                testsPassed++;
                console.log('✓ Test 1 PASSED');
            } else {
                throw new Error(`Expected 10 lines starting with line 1, got ${lines.length} lines`);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('✗ Test 1 FAILED:', error.message);
        }
        
        // Test 2: Read from middle with offset
        console.log('\n=== Test 2: Read from offset 500, length 5 ===');
        try {
            const result2 = await readFileInternal(TEST_FILE_PATH, 500, 5);
            console.log('Result length:', result2.length);
            console.log('First few lines of result:');
            console.log(result2.substring(0, 200) + '...');
            
            // Validate the result
            const lines = result2.split('\n').filter(line => line.length > 0);
            if (lines.length === 5 && lines[0].includes('line 501')) {
                testsPassed++;
                console.log('✓ Test 2 PASSED');
            } else {
                throw new Error(`Expected 5 lines starting with line 501, got ${lines.length} lines, first line: ${lines[0]}`);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('✗ Test 2 FAILED:', error.message);
        }
        
        // Test 3: Read last 10 lines (using positive offset: 1490 for lines 1491-1500)
        console.log('\n=== Test 3: Read last 10 lines (lines 1491-1500) ===');
        try {
            const result3 = await readFileInternal(TEST_FILE_PATH, 1490, 10);
            console.log('Result length:', result3.length);
            console.log('First few lines of result:');
            console.log(result3.substring(0, 300) + '...');
            
            // Validate the result
            const lines = result3.split('\n').filter(line => line.length > 0);
            if (lines.length === 10 && lines[0].includes('line 1491') && lines[9].includes('line 1500')) {
                testsPassed++;
                console.log('✓ Test 3 PASSED');
            } else {
                throw new Error(`Expected 10 lines from 1491 to 1500, got ${lines.length} lines, first line: ${lines[0]}, last line: ${lines[lines.length-1]}`);
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('✗ Test 3 FAILED:', error.message);
        }
        
        // Summary
        console.log(`\n=== SUMMARY ===`);
        console.log(`Tests passed: ${testsPassed}/${totalTests}`);
        
        if (testsPassed === totalTests) {
            console.log('🎉 All tests PASSED!');
            process.exit(0);
        } else {
            console.log('❌ Some tests FAILED!');
            process.exit(1);
        }
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('❌ Test suite failed with error:', error);
        process.exit(1);
    }
}

===== BEGIN SOURCE test/test_improved_search_truncation.js | original-bytes=5218 | sha256=973dda36800e31a35be203b27db15dd6c5153b16f26c0bdc4b3201569f90645e =====
// Test script to verify improved search result behavior using new streaming API
import { handleStartSearch, handleGetMoreSearchResults, handleStopSearch } from '../dist/handlers/search-handlers.js';

/**
 * Helper function to wait for search completion and get all results
 */
async function searchAndWaitForCompletion(searchArgs, timeout = 30000) {
  const result = await handleStartSearch(searchArgs);
  
  // Extract session ID from result with tighter regex
  const sessionIdMatch = result.content[0].text.match(/Started .* session:\s*([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
  if (!sessionIdMatch) {
    throw new Error('Could not extract session ID from search result');
  }
  const sessionId = sessionIdMatch[1];
  
  try {
    // Wait for completion by polling
    const startTime = Date.now();
    while (Date.now() - startTime < timeout) {
      const moreResults = await handleGetMoreSearchResults({ sessionId });
      
      if (moreResults.content[0].text.includes('✅ Search completed')) {
        return { initialResult: result, finalResult: moreResults, sessionId };
      }
      
      if (moreResults.content[0].text.includes('❌ ERROR')) {
        throw new Error(`Search failed: ${moreResults.content[0].text}`);
      }
      
      // Wait a bit before polling again
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
    }
    
    throw new Error('Search timed out');
  } finally {
    // Always stop the search session to prevent hanging
    try {
      await handleStopSearch({ sessionId });
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore errors when stopping - session might already be completed
    }
  }
}

async function testImprovedSearchTruncation() {
    try {
        console.log('Testing improved search result behavior with streaming API...');
        
        // Test search that will produce many results to trigger potential limits
        const searchArgs = {
            path: '.',
            pattern: '.',  // Match almost every line - this should be a lot of results
            searchType: 'content',
            maxResults: 50000,  // Very high limit to get lots of results, but may be capped
            ignoreCase: true
        };
        
        console.log('Searching for "." to get maximum results...');
        const start = Date.now();
        const { initialResult, finalResult } = await searchAndWaitForCompletion(searchArgs);
        const end = Date.now();
        
        console.log(`Search completed in ${end - start}ms`);
        console.log('Initial result type:', typeof initialResult.content[0].text);
        console.log('Initial result length:', initialResult.content[0].text.length);
        console.log('Final result type:', typeof finalResult.content[0].text);
        console.log('Final result length:', finalResult.content[0].text.length);
        
        const totalLength = initialResult.content[0].text.length + finalResult.content[0].text.length;
        const apiLimit = 1048576; // 1 MiB - use consistent constant
        
        // Check if we're within the safe limits using single source of truth
        if (totalLength > apiLimit) {
            console.log('❌ Results still quite large - over 1MB combined');
        } else if (totalLength > Math.floor(0.8 * apiLimit)) {
            console.log('⚠️  Results approaching limits but acceptable');
        } else {
            console.log('✅ Results well within safe limits');
        }
        
        if (finalResult.content[0].text.includes('Results truncated')) {
            console.log('✅ Results properly truncated with warning message');
            const truncationIndex = finalResult.content[0].text.indexOf('Results truncated');
            console.log('Truncation message:', finalResult.content[0].text.substring(truncationIndex, truncationIndex + 150));
        } else {
            console.log('ℹ️  Results complete, no truncation needed with new streaming API');
        }
        
        console.log('First 200 characters of final result:');
        console.log(finalResult.content[0].text.substring(0, 200));
        
        // Check character length safety
        const safetyMargin = apiLimit - totalLength;
        console.log(`\n📊 Safety Analysis:`);
        console.log(`   Initial response: ${initialResult.content[0].text.length.toLocaleString()} characters`);
        console.log(`   Final response: ${finalResult.content[0].text.length.toLocaleString()} characters`);
        console.log(`   Combined size: ${totalLength.toLocaleString()} characters`);
        console.log(`   API limit: ${apiLimit.toLocaleString()} characters`);
        console.log(`   Safety margin: ${safetyMargin.toLocaleString()} characters`);
        console.log(`   Utilization: ${((totalLength / apiLimit) * 100).toFixed(1)}%`);
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Test failed:', error);
        throw error;
    }
}

testImprovedSearchTruncation().then(() => {
    console.log('Improved search truncation test completed successfully.');
    process.exit(0);
}).catch(error => {
    console.error('Test failed:', error);
    process.exit(1);
});

===== BEGIN SOURCE test/test_search_truncation.js | original-bytes=4356 | sha256=b4e6292e2993ba571c0d8a307f51399c8529155cd5f34eea8093ea3b43a3b3ad =====
// Test script to verify search result behavior using new streaming API
import { handleStartSearch, handleGetMoreSearchResults, handleStopSearch } from '../dist/handlers/search-handlers.js';

/**
 * Helper function to wait for search completion and get all results
 */
async function searchAndWaitForCompletion(searchArgs, timeout = 30000) {
  const result = await handleStartSearch(searchArgs);
  
  // Extract session ID from result with tighter regex
  const sessionIdMatch = result.content[0].text.match(/Started .* session:\s*([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
  if (!sessionIdMatch) {
    throw new Error('Could not extract session ID from search result');
  }
  const sessionId = sessionIdMatch[1];
  
  try {
    // Wait for completion by polling
    const startTime = Date.now();
    while (Date.now() - startTime < timeout) {
      const moreResults = await handleGetMoreSearchResults({ sessionId });
      
      if (moreResults.content[0].text.includes('✅ Search completed')) {
        return { initialResult: result, finalResult: moreResults, sessionId };
      }
      
      if (moreResults.isError) {
        throw new Error(`Search failed: ${moreResults.content[0].text}`);
      }
      
      // Wait a bit before polling again
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
    }
    
    throw new Error('Search timed out');
  } finally {
    // Always stop the search session to prevent hanging
    try {
      await handleStopSearch({ sessionId });
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore errors when stopping - session might already be completed
    }
  }
}

async function testSearchTruncation() {
    try {
        console.log('Testing search result behavior with new streaming API...');
        
        // Test search that will produce many results
        const searchArgs = {
            path: '.',
            pattern: 'function|const|let|var',  // This should match many lines
            searchType: 'content',
            maxResults: 50000,  // Very high limit to get lots of results
            ignoreCase: true
        };
        
        console.log('Searching for common JavaScript patterns...');
        const { initialResult, finalResult } = await searchAndWaitForCompletion(searchArgs);
        
        console.log('Initial result type:', typeof initialResult.content[0].text);
        console.log('Initial result length:', initialResult.content[0].text.length);
        console.log('Final result type:', typeof finalResult.content[0].text);
        console.log('Final result length:', finalResult.content[0].text.length);
        
        const combinedLength = initialResult.content[0].text.length + finalResult.content[0].text.length;
        
        // Use consistent API limit constant
        const apiLimit = 1048576; // 1 MiB
        if (combinedLength > apiLimit) {
            console.log('⚠️  Combined results quite large - may need truncation handling');
        } else if (finalResult.content[0].text.includes('Results truncated')) {
            console.log('✅ Results properly truncated with warning message');
            const truncationIndex = finalResult.content[0].text.indexOf('Results truncated');
            console.log('Truncation message:', finalResult.content[0].text.substring(truncationIndex, truncationIndex + 100));
        } else {
            console.log('✅ Results manageable size, no truncation needed');
        }
        
        console.log('First 200 characters of final result:');
        console.log(finalResult.content[0].text.substring(0, 200));
        
        // Character length analysis
        console.log(`\n📊 Response Analysis:`);
        console.log(`   Initial response: ${initialResult.content[0].text.length.toLocaleString()} characters`);
        console.log(`   Final response: ${finalResult.content[0].text.length.toLocaleString()} characters`);
        console.log(`   Combined: ${combinedLength.toLocaleString()} characters`);
        
    } catch (error) {
        console.error('Test failed:', error);
        throw error;
    }
}

testSearchTruncation().then(() => {
    console.log('Search truncation test completed successfully.');
    process.exit(0);
}).catch(error => {
    console.error('Test failed:', error);
    process.exit(1);
});

  journal.record('start_process', {}, { sessionId: 'running', exitCode: null });

===== BEGIN SOURCE test/v12/device-capabilities.test.js | original-bytes=2978 | sha256=052d7cb5b285d4e4c74061e00605ef4a62ff77cf9adce3c431002b504470ce1b =====
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { GatewayDeviceChannel } from '../../dist/device/gateway-channel.js';
import { gatewayCapabilities } from '../../dist/device/gateway-tool-adapter.js';

function fixture() {
    const sent = [];
    let capabilities = [...gatewayCapabilities(), 'knowledge.search', 'knowledge.read'];
    const channel = new GatewayDeviceChannel({
        gatewayUrl: 'https://not-contacted.invalid',
        identity: { loadOrCreate: async () => ({ deviceId: 'test-owned-device', enrolled: true }), markEnrolled: async () => {} },
        adapter: { call: async () => ({ content: [] }) },
        capabilities: () => capabilities
    });
    channel.socket = { readyState: 1, send: text => sent.push(JSON.parse(text)) };
    return { channel, sent, setCapabilities: value => { capabilities = value; } };
}

test('capability lifecycle uses existing authenticated epoch-bound frame and avoids unchanged fanout', async () => {
    const f = fixture();
    assert.equal(f.channel.updateCapabilities(), false, 'No capability update before authentication');
    assert.equal(f.sent.length, 0);
    await f.channel.sendHello();
    assert(f.sent[0].payload.capabilities.includes('knowledge.search'));
    assert.equal(f.sent[0].type, 'auth_hello');

    // Same authenticated connection established by the existing proof protocol.
    f.channel.authenticatedDeviceId = 'test-owned-device';
    f.channel.connectionEpoch = 7;
    assert.equal(f.channel.updateCapabilities(), false, 'Unchanged hello metadata does not require a second frame');
    f.setCapabilities(gatewayCapabilities());
    assert.equal(f.channel.updateCapabilities(), true);
    const update = f.sent.at(-1);
    assert.equal(update.type, 'capability_sync');
    assert.equal(update.device_id, 'test-owned-device');
    assert.equal(update.connection_epoch, 7);
    assert.equal(update.protocol_version, 1);
    assert.deepEqual(update.payload.capabilities, gatewayCapabilities());
    assert.equal(f.channel.updateCapabilities(), false);
    assert.equal(f.sent.length, 2);
    assert(!JSON.stringify(update).includes('private_key'));
});

test('change while disconnected is advertised after reconnect, failed send does not lose the update', () => {
    const f = fixture();
    f.channel.authenticatedDeviceId = 'test-owned-device';
    f.channel.connectionEpoch = 8;
    f.channel.socket.readyState = 3;
    assert.equal(f.channel.updateCapabilities(), false);
    f.setCapabilities([...gatewayCapabilities(), 'example.echo']);
    f.channel.socket.readyState = 1;
    f.channel.socket.send = () => { throw new Error('transport closing'); };
    assert.equal(f.channel.updateCapabilities(), false);
    f.channel.socket.send = text => f.sent.push(JSON.parse(text));
    assert.equal(f.channel.updateCapabilities(), true);
    assert(f.sent.at(-1).payload.capabilities.includes('example.echo'));
    assert.equal(f.sent.at(-1).connection_epoch, 8);
});

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';

const thisFile = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const candidateRepo = path.resolve(path.dirname(thisFile), '../..');

function resultShape(value) {
    if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(resultShape);
    if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
        return Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(value).sort().map(key => [key, resultShape(value[key])]));
    }
    return value === null ? 'null' : typeof value;
}
const textOf = result => (result?.content ?? []).filter(item => item.type === 'text').map(item => item.text).join('\n');

async function worker() {
    const repo = path.resolve(process.env.DEVICE_CORE_PARITY_REPO);
    const root = path.resolve(process.env.DEVICE_CORE_PARITY_ROOT);
    const mode = process.env.DEVICE_CORE_PARITY_MODE;
    assert.equal(path.resolve(process.env.MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR), path.join(root, 'config'));
    const fixture = path.join(root, 'fixtures');
    await fs.mkdir(fixture, { recursive: true });

    const { configManager } = await load('config-manager.js');
    await configManager.loadConfig();
    await configManager.setValue('allowedDirectories', [fixture]);
    await configManager.setValue('telemetryEnabled', false);
    const { GatewayToolAdapter, gatewayCapabilities } = await load('device/gateway-tool-adapter.js');
    const { dispatchToolCall } = await load('tool-dispatcher.js');

        allowedRoots: [fixture], context: { deviceId: 'parity-device', stateRoot: path.join(root, 'config') }
    });
    let core;
    if (mode === 'core') {
        const { createDeviceCore } = await load('device/core.js');
        core = await createDeviceCore({ deviceId: 'parity-device', stateRoot: path.join(root, 'config'), allowedRoots: [fixture] });
        assert.match(core.runtimeInstanceId, /^direct-in-process:[0-9a-f-]{36}$/);
        assert.deepEqual(core.capabilities, gatewayCapabilities());
        await assert.rejects(createDeviceCore({ deviceId: 'bad', stateRoot: path.join(root, 'wrong') }),
            error => error.code === 'CONFIG_ROOT_MISMATCH');
    }
    const native = (name, args) => mode === 'core' ? core.invokeNative(name, args) : dispatchToolCall(name, args, { isRemote: true });
    const broker = (name, args) => mode === 'core' ? core.invoke(name, args) : adapter.call(name, args);
    const normalize = input => String(input)
        .split(root).join('<ROOT>').split(root.replaceAll('\\', '/')).join('<ROOT>')
        .replace(/Runtime: \d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:ms|s)/g, 'Runtime: <TIME>')
        .replace(/search_\d+_[a-z0-9]+/gi, '<SEARCH>')
        .replace(/search_\d+/g, '<SEARCH>');
    const snapshot = { brokerCapabilities: gatewayCapabilities(), results: {}, sideEffects: {} };
    const record = (name, result) => {
        snapshot.results[name] = { shape: resultShape(result), text: normalize(textOf(result)), isError: result.isError === true };
        return result;
    };

    const file = path.join(fixture, 'native.txt');
    assert.notEqual((await native('write_file', { path: file, content: 'alpha\r\nbeta\r\nalpha\r\n', mode: 'rewrite' })).isError, true);
    record('selectedRead', await native('read_file', { path: file, offset: 1, length: 1 }));
    assert.match(snapshot.results.selectedRead.text, /beta/);
    await native('write_file', { path: file, content: 'gamma\r\n', mode: 'append' });
    assert.equal(await fs.readFile(file, 'utf8'), 'alpha\r\nbeta\r\nalpha\r\ngamma\r\n');
    record('batchEdit', await native('edit_block', {
        file_path: file, edits: [
            { old_string: 'alpha', new_string: 'ALPHA', expected_replacements: 2 },
            { old_string: 'beta', new_string: 'BETA', expected_replacements: 1 }
        ]
    }));
    const beforeReject = await fs.readFile(file);
    const rejected = record('batchAtomicFailure', await native('edit_block', {
        file_path: file, edits: [
            { old_string: 'BETA', new_string: 'changed', expected_replacements: 1 },
            { old_string: 'missing-exact-text', new_string: 'bad', expected_replacements: 1 }
        ]
    }));
    assert.equal(rejected.isError, true);
    assert.deepEqual(await fs.readFile(file), beforeReject);
    snapshot.sideEffects.textBytes = beforeReject.toString('base64');
    record('multipleRead', await native('read_multiple_files', { paths: [file, path.join(fixture, 'missing.txt')] }));

    const brokerFile = path.join(fixture, 'broker.txt');
    await broker('write_file', { path: brokerFile, content: 'one\ntwo\nthree\n' });
    record('brokerSelectedRead', await broker('read_text_file', { path: brokerFile, offset: 1, length: 1 }));
    const beforeDryRun = await fs.readFile(brokerFile);
    record('brokerDryRun', await broker('edit_file', { path: brokerFile, old_text: 'not-present', new_text: 'different', dry_run: true }));
    assert.deepEqual(await fs.readFile(brokerFile), beforeDryRun);
    await broker('edit_file', { path: brokerFile, old_text: 'two', new_text: 'TWO' });
    snapshot.sideEffects.brokerText = await fs.readFile(brokerFile, 'utf8');
    await assert.rejects(broker('read_text_file', { path: path.join(root, 'outside.txt') }));
    await assert.rejects(broker('read_process_output', { session_id: '01' }));

    const ExcelJS = requireFromRepo('exceljs');

    const excel = path.join(fixture, 'sample.xlsx');
    await workbook.xlsx.writeFile(excel);
    record('excelRangeRead', await native('read_file', { path: excel, range: "'Sheet One'!A1:B2" }));
    assert.match(snapshot.results.excelRangeRead.text, /kept/);
    record('excelRangeEdit', await native('edit_block', { file_path: excel, range: "'Sheet One'!B2:B2", content: [[99]] }));
    const changed = new ExcelJS.Workbook();
    await changed.xlsx.readFile(excel);
    assert.equal(changed.getWorksheet('Sheet One').getCell('B2').value, 99);
    assert.equal(changed.getWorksheet('Sheet One').getCell('B3').value, 30);
    snapshot.sideEffects.excel = [changed.getWorksheet('Sheet One').getCell('B2').value, changed.getWorksheet('Sheet One').getCell('B3').value];

    const PizZip = requireFromRepo('pizzip');
    const zip = new PizZip();
    zip.file('[Content_Types].xml', '<?xml version="1.0"?><Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types"><Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/><Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/><Override PartName="/word/document.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document.main+xml"/></Types>');
    zip.file('_rels/.rels', '<?xml version="1.0"?><Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships"><Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument" Target="word/document.xml"/></Relationships>');
    zip.file('word/document.xml', '<?xml version="1.0"?><w:document xmlns:w="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/wordprocessingml/2006/main"><w:body><w:p><w:r><w:t>DOCX original marker</w:t></w:r></w:p><w:p><w:r><w:t>Keep this paragraph</w:t></w:r></w:p></w:body></w:document>');
    const docx = path.join(fixture, 'sample.docx');
    await fs.writeFile(docx, zip.generate({ type: 'nodebuffer' }));
    record('docxRead', await native('read_file', { path: docx }));
    assert.match(snapshot.results.docxRead.text, /DOCX original marker/);
    record('docxEdit', await native('edit_block', { file_path: docx, old_string: 'DOCX original marker', new_string: 'DOCX verified marker', expected_replacements: 1 }));
    const reopened = new PizZip(await fs.readFile(docx));
    const xml = reopened.file('word/document.xml').asText();
    assert.match(xml, /DOCX verified marker/);
    assert.match(xml, /Keep this paragraph/);
    snapshot.sideEffects.docx = xml;

    const { PDFDocument, StandardFonts } = requireFromRepo('pdf-lib');

    await fs.mkdir(searchRoot, { recursive: true });
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(searchRoot, 'match.txt'), 'prefix\nneedle.parity literal\nsuffix\n');
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(searchRoot, 'skip.log'), 'needle.parity should be excluded\n');
    const started = await native('start_search', {
        path: searchRoot, pattern: 'needle.parity', searchType: 'content',
        filePattern: '*.txt', literalSearch: true, contextLines: 0, timeout_ms: 5000
    });
    assert.notEqual(started.isError, true);
    const session = textOf(started).match(/session: (\S+)/)?.[1];
    assert(session, textOf(started));
    let page;
    for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
        page = await native('get_more_search_results', { sessionId: session, offset: 0, length: 10 });
        if (textOf(page).includes('Status: COMPLETED')) break;
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
    }
    assert.match(textOf(page), /Status: COMPLETED/);
    assert.match(textOf(page), /match\.txt/);
    assert.doesNotMatch(textOf(page), /skip\.log/);

    const processResult = await broker('start_process', { command, working_directory: fixture, timeout_ms: 1000 });
    assert.notEqual(processResult.isError, true);
    assert.match(String(processResult.session_id), /^[1-9]\d*$/);
    const output = await broker('read_process_output', { session_id: processResult.session_id, offset: 0, length: 50 });
    assert.match(textOf(output), /process-first/);
    assert.match(textOf(output), /process-last/);
    const outputAgain = await broker('read_process_output', { session_id: processResult.session_id, offset: 0, length: 50 });
    assert.match(textOf(outputAgain), /process-first/);
    snapshot.results.retainedProcess = {
        startedShape: resultShape(processResult),
        outputShape: resultShape(output),
        retainedFirst: textOf(outputAgain).includes('process-first'),
        retainedLast: textOf(outputAgain).includes('process-last')
    };
    const shell = await broker('shell_execute', { command, working_directory: fixture, timeout_ms: 3000 });
    assert.equal(shell.exitCode, 0);

    snapshot.results.shell = { shape: resultShape(shell), exitCode: shell.exitCode, stdout: shell.stdout, stderr: shell.stderr, timedOut: shell.timedOut };

    if (core) {

        await core.close();

        await assert.rejects(core.invoke('read_text_file', { path: file }), error => error.code === 'DEVICE_NOT_READY');
        await assert.rejects(core.invokeNative('read_file', { path: file }), error => error.code === 'DEVICE_NOT_READY');
    }
    await fs.writeFile(process.env.DEVICE_CORE_PARITY_RECEIPT, JSON.stringify(snapshot, null, 2) + '\n');
}

async function runWorker(repo, root, mode) {
    const receipt = path.join(root, 'snapshot.json');
    await fs.mkdir(root, { recursive: true });
    const childEnv = {
        ...process.env,
        MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR: path.join(root, 'config'),
        MCP_DEVICE_REMOTE: 'false',
        MCP_DEVICE_DISABLE_TELEMETRY: 'true',
        DEVICE_CORE_PARITY_WORKER: '1',
        DEVICE_CORE_PARITY_REPO: repo,
        DEVICE_CORE_PARITY_ROOT: root,
        DEVICE_CORE_PARITY_MODE: mode,
        DEVICE_CORE_PARITY_RECEIPT: receipt,
        TMP: root, TEMP: root, TMPDIR: root
    };
    const outcome = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const child = spawn(process.execPath, [thisFile], { cwd: root, env: childEnv, windowsHide: true });
        let stdout = '', stderr = '';
        const timer = setTimeout(() => { child.kill(); reject(new Error('Parity worker timed out')); }, 90000);
        child.stdout.on('data', data => { stdout += data; });
        child.stderr.on('data', data => { stderr += data; });
        child.on('error', error => { clearTimeout(timer); reject(error); });
        child.on('close', code => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve({ code, stdout, stderr }); });
    });
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(root, 'stdout.log'), outcome.stdout);
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(root, 'stderr.log'), outcome.stderr);
    assert.equal(outcome.code, 0, outcome.stderr + '\n' + outcome.stdout.slice(-6000));
    return JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(receipt, 'utf8'));
}

if (process.env.DEVICE_CORE_PARITY_WORKER === '1') {
    worker().then(() => process.exit(0)).catch(error => { console.error(error); process.exit(1); });
} else {
    test('shared Device Core preserves native and broker behavior against original v11 dist', { timeout: 240000 }, async () => {
        const baseline = process.env.DEVICE_PARITY_BASELINE_REPO;
        assert(baseline, 'Set DEVICE_PARITY_BASELINE_REPO to the verified original checkout (never silently substitute candidate)');
        const outputRoot = process.env.DEVICE_PARITY_OUTPUT_ROOT
            ? path.resolve(process.env.DEVICE_PARITY_OUTPUT_ROOT)
            : await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-device-core-parity-'));
        const oldResult = await runWorker(path.resolve(baseline), path.join(outputRoot, 'v11'), 'legacy');
        const newResult = await runWorker(candidateRepo, path.join(outputRoot, 'v12'), 'core');
        assert.deepEqual(newResult, oldResult, 'Behavior/result-shape/side-effect difference against v11');
        console.log('Parity snapshots: ' + outputRoot);
    });
}

===== BEGIN SOURCE test/v12/knowledge-correction-guards.test.js | original-bytes=5664 | sha256=5f03ac72162907702890d9bb043299678014826277929e877e05b2ac408ac539 =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { KnowledgeRouter } from '../../dist/platform/knowledge-router.js';

function fixture() {
  const capabilities=['knowledge.search','knowledge.read','knowledge.stat','knowledge.import'];
  const devices=[{deviceId:'A',accountId:'owner',online:true,capabilities},{deviceId:'B',accountId:'owner',online:true,capabilities}];
  const old={id:'K',version:1,contentHash:'revision-1',summary:'Shared fix',body:'Original shared body.',status:'observed',originDeviceId:'B',servingDeviceId:'B',scope:{visibility:'account'},evidence:[],derivedFrom:[],provenance:['B'],updatedAt:1,lastVerifiedAt:1};
  const state={preview:structuredClone(old),local:{...structuredClone(old),servingDeviceId:'A'},origin:structuredClone(old),metadata:undefined,readError:undefined,statError:undefined};
  const imports=[],calls=[];
  const transport={
    async devices(){return devices;},
    async call(accountId,deviceId,capability,input){
      calls.push({accountId,deviceId,capability});
      if(capability==='knowledge.search')return{items:[state.preview]};
      if(capability==='knowledge.stat'){if(state.statError)throw Object.assign(new Error(state.statError),{code:state.statError});return state.metadata??state.origin;}
      if(capability==='knowledge.import'){imports.push(structuredClone(input.card));return{imported:true};}
      if(deviceId==='A')return{card:structuredClone(state.local)};
      if(state.readError)throw Object.assign(new Error(state.readError),{code:state.readError});
      return{card:structuredClone(state.origin)};
    }
  };
  const router=new KnowledgeRouter(transport,{secret:Buffer.alloc(32,7)});
  const context={accountId:'owner',requesterDeviceId:'A',sessionId:'fixture'};
  const search=()=>router.search(context,{providerDeviceId:'B'});
  const revision=(values={})=>({...structuredClone(old),version:2,contentHash:'revision-2',updatedAt:2,...values});
  return{router,context,search,state,imports,calls,devices,revision};
}

test('origin scope revocation cannot return/import the old local or cached card',async()=>{
  const f=fixture(),found=await f.search(),ref=found.items[0].read_ref;
  await f.router.read(f.context,{ref});f.imports.length=0;
  f.state.origin=f.revision({scope:{visibility:'local'}});
  await assert.rejects(f.router.read(f.context,{ref}),{code:'ACCESS_DENIED'});
  assert.equal(f.imports.length,0);assert.equal(f.router.cacheStats().entries,0);
  f.devices[1].online=false;
  await assert.rejects(f.router.read(f.context,{ref}),{code:'ACCESS_DENIED'});
  assert.equal(f.imports.length,0);
});

test('known correction read failure is explicit and its revision floor survives the next offline read',async()=>{
  const f=fixture(),found=await f.search(),ref=found.items[0].read_ref;
  await f.router.read(f.context,{ref});f.imports.length=0;
  f.state.origin=f.revision({status:'withdrawn'});f.state.readError='KNOWLEDGE_BUDGET_EXCEEDED';
  await assert.rejects(f.router.read(f.context,{ref}),{code:'KNOWLEDGE_BUDGET_EXCEEDED'});

  f.devices[1].online=false;
  await assert.rejects(f.router.read(f.context,{ref}),{code:'KNOWLEDGE_REVISION_UNAVAILABLE'});
  assert.equal(f.imports.length,0);
  f.devices[1].online=true;f.state.readError=undefined;
  const corrected=await f.router.read(f.context,{ref});
  assert.equal(corrected.card.version,2);assert.equal(corrected.card.status,'withdrawn');
  assert.equal(f.imports.at(-1).status,'withdrawn');
});

===== BEGIN SOURCE test/v12/knowledge-router.test.js | original-bytes=6443 | sha256=6afaabcb3a1df8296301039276ad26f4419e5bef5c727908179f286fd39c9899 =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { KnowledgeRouter } from '../../dist/platform/knowledge-router.js';

function network() {
  const capabilities = ['knowledge.search','knowledge.list','knowledge.read','knowledge.stat','knowledge.import'];
  const devices = [{ deviceId:'A',accountId:'owner',online:true,capabilities,projects:[] },{ deviceId:'B',accountId:'owner',online:true,capabilities,projects:['project'] },{ deviceId:'C',accountId:'owner',online:true,capabilities:[] },{ deviceId:'D',accountId:'other',online:true,capabilities }];
  const card = { id:'K',version:1,contentHash:'revision-1',summary:'Fix ERR_LOCK with a transaction',body:'Observed transaction repair with a checked postcondition.',status:'observed',originDeviceId:'B',servingDeviceId:'B',scope:{projectId:'project',visibility:'project'},evidence:[{id:'original-evidence',originDeviceId:'B',sourceId:'source',sourceRef:'source-ref',recordHash:'source-hash',kind:'source_trace',sourceAvailable:true,observedAt:1}],derivedFrom:[],provenance:['B'],updatedAt:1,lastVerifiedAt:1 };
  const stores = new Map([['A',new Map()],['B',new Map([['K',structuredClone(card)]])],['D',new Map()]]), calls = [];
  const transport = { async devices(){ return devices; }, async call(accountId,deviceId,capability,input){
    calls.push({accountId,deviceId,capability,input}); const device=devices.find(d=>d.deviceId===deviceId);
    if(!device || device.accountId!==accountId) throw Object.assign(new Error('owner'),{code:'ACCESS_DENIED'});
    if(!device.online) throw new Error('offline'); const store=stores.get(deviceId);
    if(capability==='knowledge.search'||capability==='knowledge.list') return {items:[...store.values()].map(c=>({...c,evidenceCount:c.evidence.length,read_ref:c.id}))};
    if(capability==='knowledge.import'){const prior=store.get(input.card.id);store.set(input.card.id,{...structuredClone(input.card),servingDeviceId:deviceId});return{imported:!prior||prior.version!==input.card.version};}
    const found=store.get(input.ref); if(!found) throw Object.assign(new Error('missing'),{code:'KNOWLEDGE_NOT_FOUND'});
    return capability==='knowledge.stat'?{id:found.id,version:found.version,contentHash:found.contentHash,status:found.status}:{card:structuredClone(found)};
  }};
  const router=new KnowledgeRouter(transport,{secret:Buffer.alloc(32,7)}), ctx=(requesterDeviceId='C',accountId='owner')=>({accountId,requesterDeviceId,sessionId:'session'});
  return {router,devices,stores,calls,card,ctx};
}

test('C without Palace searches one relevant provider and reads with bounded session cache only',async()=>{
  const {router,stores,calls,ctx}=network(); const search=await router.search(ctx(),{query:'ERR_LOCK',projectId:'project'});
  assert.equal(search.items.length,1);assert.deepEqual(search.queried,['B']);assert.equal(calls.filter(c=>c.capability==='knowledge.import').length,0);
  const read=await router.read(ctx(),{ref:search.items[0].read_ref});assert.equal(read.card.id,'K');assert.equal(read.imported,false);assert.equal(stores.has('C'),false);
  assert.equal(router.cacheStats().entries,1);const before=calls.filter(c=>c.capability==='knowledge.read').length;
  await router.read(ctx(),{ref:search.items[0].read_ref});assert.equal(calls.filter(c=>c.capability==='knowledge.read').length,before);assert.equal(calls.filter(c=>c.capability==='knowledge.import').length,0);
});

test('search/list never import; selected read imports once with original evidence identity',async()=>{
  const {router,stores,calls,ctx}=network();const search=await router.search(ctx('A'),{query:'ERR_LOCK',projectId:'project'});
  await router.search(ctx('A'),{projectId:'project'},'list');assert.equal(stores.get('A').size,0);
  await router.read(ctx('A'),{ref:search.items[0].read_ref});assert.equal(stores.get('A').size,1);assert.equal(stores.get('A').get('K').originDeviceId,'B');
  assert.deepEqual(stores.get('A').get('K').evidence.map(e=>e.id),['original-evidence']);
  const fromA=await router.search(ctx(),{projectId:'project',providerDeviceId:'A'});await router.read(ctx(),{ref:fromA.items[0].read_ref});
  assert.equal(calls.filter(c=>c.deviceId==='C'&&c.capability==='knowledge.import').length,0);
});

test('on-demand origin correction updates imported copy; offline origin is explicitly stale',async()=>{
  const {router,stores,devices,calls,ctx}=network();const search=await router.search(ctx('A'),{projectId:'project'},'list');await router.read(ctx('A'),{ref:search.items[0].read_ref});
  Object.assign(stores.get('B').get('K'),{version:2,contentHash:'revision-2',status:'withdrawn'});
  const corrected=await router.read(ctx('A'),{ref:search.items[0].read_ref});assert.equal(corrected.card.status,'withdrawn');assert.equal(stores.get('A').get('K').version,2);
  devices.find(d=>d.deviceId==='B').online=false;const before=calls.length;
  const offline=await router.read(ctx('A'),{ref:search.items[0].read_ref});assert.equal(offline.freshness.status,'stale');assert.equal(offline.card.version,2);
  assert.ok(calls.slice(before).every(c=>c.deviceId==='A'));
});

test('another account, tampered refs, wrong requester and project boundaries are rejected',async()=>{
  const {router,ctx,calls}=network();const search=await router.search(ctx(),{projectId:'project'});const ref=search.items[0].read_ref;

  await assert.rejects(router.read(ctx(),{ref,projectId:'private-other'}),{code:'ACCESS_DENIED'});
  const none=await router.search(ctx(),{projectId:'different'});assert.equal(none.items.length,0);
  assert.equal(calls.some(c=>c.accountId==='other'&&c.deviceId==='B'),false);
});

test('non-loopback HTTP OAuth issuer is rejected unless LAN HTTP is explicitly enabled', () => {
  const issuerUrl = new URL('http://abc:8101');
  const resourceServerUrl = new URL('http://abc:8101/mcp');
  assert.throws(
    () => createOAuthAuthorizationServerMetadata({ provider, issuerUrl, resourceServerUrl }),
    /Issuer URL must be HTTPS/
  );
  const metadata = createOAuthAuthorizationServerMetadata({
    provider,
    issuerUrl,
    resourceServerUrl,
    dangerouslyAllowInsecureIssuerUrl: true
  });
  assert.equal(metadata.issuer, 'http://abc:8101/');
  assert.equal(metadata.authorization_endpoint, 'http://abc:8101/authorize');
  assert.equal(metadata.token_endpoint, 'http://abc:8101/token');
  assert.equal(metadata.registration_endpoint, 'http://abc:8101/register');
  assert.equal(metadata.revocation_endpoint, 'http://abc:8101/revoke');
});

Loopback traffic uses the same account authentication and authorization checks as remote traffic.', query:'Why is a localhost MCP connection rejected with HTTP 401?'},

function treeBytes(root) {
  let bytes=0;
  for(const entry of readdirSync(root,{withFileTypes:true})){
    const path=join(root,entry.name);
    if(entry.isDirectory())bytes+=treeBytes(path);else if(entry.isFile())bytes+=statSync(path).size;
  }
  return bytes;
}

test('held-out scoped retrieval benchmark reports quality, stale errors, bytes, latency and storage',async t=>{
  const root=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'mcp-v12-palace-benchmark-'));
  const projects=['orders','gateway','assets'],projectRoots=projects.map(p=>join(root,p));
  const dataRoot=join(root,'provider'),deviceId='benchmark-device';
  const rawBytes={};let sourceRecords=0;
  for(const project of projects){
    const logRoot=join(root,project,'.mcp-palace');mkdirSync(logRoot,{recursive:true});
    const records=[...corpus.filter(x=>x.project===project).map(x=>x.body)];
    for(let batch=0;batch<3;batch++)for(let i=0;i<distractorTopics.length;i++)
      records.push(distractorTopics[i]+' Incident '+(batch*12+i+1)+' in '+project+' archive.');
    const text=records.join('\n')+'\n';
    rawBytes[project]=Buffer.byteLength(text);sourceRecords+=records.length;
    writeFileSync(join(logRoot,'session.log'),text);
  }
  const stalePath=join(projectRoots[1],'.mcp-palace','old-claim.log');
  writeFileSync(stalePath,'Error DEPRECATED_ROUTE_CACHE: this obsolete route workaround must be withdrawn after deleting its source.\n');
  sourceRecords++;
  const provider=createPalaceProvider({dataRoot,deviceId,projectRoots,sourceRoots:[],autoIndex:false});
  await provider.start();
  t.after(async()=>{await provider.stop();rmSync(root,{recursive:true,force:true});});
  const indexStart=performance.now();let ingestRounds=0,created=0;
  for(let i=0;i<6;i++){
    const receipt=await provider.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{maxRecords:256,maxFiles:32,maxBytes:4*1024*1024});
    created+=receipt.created;ingestRounds++;
    if(receipt.complete)break;
  }
  const indexMs=performance.now()-indexStart;
  const expected=new Map();
  for(const project of projects){
    const list=await provider.invoke('knowledge.list',{projectId:project,limit:50,maxBytes:65536});
    for(const item of list.items)for(const record of corpus.filter(x=>x.project===project))
      if(item.summary.includes(record.body.slice(0,70)))expected.set(record.key,item.id);
  }
  assert.equal(expected.size,corpus.length,'Every target must actually have been indexed before measuring retrieval');
  const latencies=[],responses=[];let top1=0,hit3=0,relevantReturned=0,totalReturned=0,scopeLeaks=0,budgetErrors=0;
  for(const request of corpus){
    const start=performance.now();
    const response=await provider.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:request.query,projectId:request.project,limit:3,maxBytes:4096});
    latencies.push(performance.now()-start);
    const bytes=Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(response));
    const ids=response.items.map(item=>item.id),wanted=expected.get(request.key);
    if(ids[0]===wanted)top1++;if(ids.includes(wanted))hit3++;
    relevantReturned+=ids.filter(id=>id===wanted).length;totalReturned+=ids.length;
    scopeLeaks+=response.items.filter(item=>item.scope.visibility==='project'&&item.scope.projectId!==request.project).length;
    if(bytes>4096)budgetErrors++;
    responses.push({key:request.key,query:request.query,top1:ids[0]===wanted,hit3:ids.includes(wanted),returned:ids.length,bytes,
      retrievedSummaries:response.items.map(x=>x.summary)});
  }
  // Warm latency samples do not alter quality scores or learn from the answers.
  for(let round=0;round<5;round++)for(const request of corpus){
    const start=performance.now();
    await provider.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:request.query,projectId:request.project,limit:3,maxBytes:4096});
    latencies.push(performance.now()-start);
  }
  const negativeResults=[];
  for(const request of noAnswerRequests){
    const response=await provider.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:request.query,projectId:request.project,limit:3,maxBytes:4096});
    negativeResults.push({...request,returned:response.items.length,summaries:response.items.map(x=>x.summary)});
  }
  const heldoutRequests=[
    {project:'orders',key:'sqlite-lock',query:'The settlement database is busy and a write must be retried. Which lock handling preserves idempotency?'},
    {project:'orders',key:'cursor',query:'We see missing orders while scrolling as fulfillment changes. Which stable pagination fields should drive the cursor?'},
    {project:'gateway',key:'auth-loopback',query:'MCP from the same machine receives unauthorized. Is account pairing still part of the connection setup?'},
    {project:'gateway',key:'proxy',query:'The site opens, but upgraded socket traffic never reaches the paired device. What must the proxy forward?'},
    {project:'assets',key:'backup',query:'How do we relocate persisted knowledge with enough evidence to audit it after recovery?'},
    {project:'gateway',key:'port',query:'A test gateway cannot bind its socket because another service owns the address; how do we investigate safely?'}
  ];
  const heldoutCases=[];
  for(const request of heldoutRequests){
    const response=await provider.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:request.query,projectId:request.project,limit:3,maxBytes:4096});
    const ids=response.items.map(item=>item.id),wanted=expected.get(request.key);
    heldoutCases.push({...request,top1:ids[0]===wanted,hit3:ids.includes(wanted),returned:ids.length,
      summaries:response.items.map(item=>item.summary)});
  }
  const before=(await provider.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:'DEPRECATED_ROUTE_CACHE',projectId:'gateway'})).items;
  assert.equal(before.length,1);
  rmSync(stalePath);
  for(let i=0;i<4;i++)await provider.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{maxRecords:256,maxFiles:32,maxBytes:4*1024*1024});
  const staleErrors=(await provider.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:'DEPRECATED_ROUTE_CACHE',projectId:'gateway'})).items.length;
  const staleStat=await provider.invoke('knowledge.stat',{ref:before[0].id});
  const storageBeforeStop=treeBytes(dataRoot);
  await provider.stop();
  const result={
    benchmark:'palace-scoped-heldout-v1',node:process.version,platform:process.platform,sourceRecords,targetRequests:corpus.length,distractors:sourceRecords-corpus.length-1,
    method:'Fixed natural-language troubleshooting requests, separate expected IDs, 3 project scopes, no generation and no answer labels in provider inputs.',
    quality:{top1:top1/corpus.length,hitAt3:hit3/corpus.length,precisionAt3:relevantReturned/(3*corpus.length),precisionAmongReturnedAtMost3:relevantReturned/Math.max(1,totalReturned),
      noAnswerAbstention:negativeResults.filter(x=>x.returned===0).length/noAnswerRequests.length,
      scopeLeaks,staleErrors,budgetErrors,staleStatus:staleStat.status},
    efficiency:{indexMs,ingestRounds,created,searchSamples:latencies.length,p50Ms:percentile(latencies,.5),p95Ms:percentile(latencies,.95),
      previewMeanBytes:responses.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.bytes,0)/responses.length,
      rawProjectMeanBytes:Object.values(rawBytes).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/projects.length,
      storageBeforeStopBytes:storageBeforeStop,storageCheckpointedBytes:treeBytes(dataRoot),modelCalls:0},
    cases:responses,noAnswerCases:negativeResults,
    heldout:{method:'Six additional paraphrases fixed after implementation; results do not tune the implementation.',
      top1:heldoutCases.filter(c=>c.top1).length/heldoutCases.length,hitAt3:heldoutCases.filter(c=>c.hit3).length/heldoutCases.length,cases:heldoutCases},
    limits:['Fixture relevance measurement is not downstream task success.','Bytes are measured, not model tokens.',
      'Initial 12-case fixture became a regression set after it exposed common-word noise; separate paraphrases remain held out.','No semantic embedding/model retrieval is claimed.','Latency excludes connector and network overhead.','Managed state is measured; external source bytes are excluded.']
  };
  console.log('PALACE_BENCHMARK '+JSON.stringify(result));
  if(process.env.MCP_V12_PALACE_BENCHMARK_OUTPUT)writeFileSync(process.env.MCP_V12_PALACE_BENCHMARK_OUTPUT,JSON.stringify(result,null,2)+'\n');
  assert.equal(scopeLeaks,0);assert.equal(staleErrors,0);assert.equal(budgetErrors,0);assert.equal(staleStat.status,'stale');
});

import test from 'node:test';

import { createPalaceProvider } from '../../dist/plugins/palace/index.js';
import { KnowledgeRouter } from '../../dist/platform/knowledge-router.js';
import { ActivityJournal } from '../../dist/platform/activity-journal.js';

async function fixture(t) {
  const root=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'mcp-v12-palace-platform-'));
  const runtime=join(root,'runtime'),logs=join(runtime,'logs');mkdirSync(logs,{recursive:true});
  const providers=[];
  const open=async(deviceId,sourceRoots=[logs])=>{
    const p=createPalaceProvider({deviceId,dataRoot:join(root,'provider-'+deviceId),sourceRoots,autoIndex:false});
    await p.start();providers.push(p);return p;
  };
  t.after(async()=>{for(const p of providers)await p.stop();rmSync(root,{recursive:true,force:true});});
  return{root,runtime,logs,open};
}

test('retained activity segments remain discoverable and exact evidence survives rollover without path churn',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t);
  const initial=new ActivityJournal(f.runtime,'B','initial-fixture',400);
  initial.record('repair-fixture-selected',{path:join(f.root,'selected.txt')},{exitCode:0});
  await initial.close();
  const selectedSegment=readdirSync(f.logs)[0],selectedPath=join(f.logs,selectedSegment);
  assert.match(selectedSegment,/^device-activity-\d+-[0-9a-f-]+\.jsonl$/);
  const originalBytes=readFileSync(selectedPath);
  const b=await f.open('B');
  await b.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{maxFiles:16,maxRecords:32,maxBytes:65536});
  const search=await b.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:'repair-fixture-selected'});
  assert.equal(search.items.length,1);
  const before=await b.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:search.items[0].id,evidence:true});
  assert.equal(before.evidence[0].available,true);assert.equal(before.evidence[0].verifiedBytes,true);
  assert.match(before.evidence[0].text,/repair-fixture-selected/);
  const next=new ActivityJournal(f.runtime,'B','rollover-fixture',400);
  for(let i=1;i<=3;i++)next.record('retained-rollover-'+i,{}, {exitCode:0});
  await next.close();
  const names=readdirSync(f.logs);
  assert.equal(names.length,4);assert.ok(names.every(name=>name.endsWith('.jsonl')));
  assert.ok(existsSync(selectedPath));assert.deepEqual(readFileSync(selectedPath),originalBytes);
  await b.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{maxFiles:16,maxRecords:32,maxBytes:65536});
  const after=await b.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:before.card.id,evidence:true});
  assert.equal(after.card.status,'observed');assert.equal(after.card.version,before.card.version);
  assert.equal(after.card.contentHash,before.card.contentHash);
  assert.equal(after.card.evidence[0].sourceRef,before.card.evidence[0].sourceRef);
  assert.equal(after.evidence[0].available,true);assert.equal(after.evidence[0].text,before.evidence[0].text);
  assert.equal((await b.invoke('knowledge.list',{})).items.length,4);
  assert.equal((await b.invoke('knowledge.skills',{})).items.length,0);
});

for(const mutation of ['rewrite','delete'])test('selected federation read revalidates origin evidence after source '+mutation+' without background ingest',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t),source=join(f.logs,'origin.jsonl');
  writeFileSync(source,JSON.stringify({toolName:'fixture-runner',arguments:{},output:'Error FRESH_MARKER: preserve the atomic idempotency key.'})+'\n');
  const a=await f.open('A',[]),b=await f.open('B');
  await b.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});
  const capabilities=['knowledge.search','knowledge.list','knowledge.read','knowledge.stat','knowledge.import'];
  const devices=[{deviceId:'A',accountId:'owner',online:true,capabilities},{deviceId:'B',accountId:'owner',online:true,capabilities}];
  const providers={A:a,B:b},calls=[];
  const router=new KnowledgeRouter({
    async devices(){return devices;},
    async call(accountId,deviceId,capability,input){calls.push({accountId,deviceId,capability});return providers[deviceId].invoke(capability,input);}
  },{secret:Buffer.alloc(32,23)});
  const context={accountId:'owner',requesterDeviceId:'A',sessionId:'freshness-fixture'};
  const found=await router.search(context,{query:'FRESH_MARKER',providerDeviceId:'B'});
  assert.equal(found.items.length,1);
  const first=await router.read(context,{ref:found.items[0].read_ref});
  assert.equal(first.card.status,'observed');assert.equal(first.imported,true);
  const dependent=await a.invoke('knowledge.learn',{summary:'Atomic key procedure',steps:['Preserve the idempotency key'],evidenceRefs:[first.card.id]});
  assert.equal(dependent.card.status,'hypothesis');
  if(mutation==='delete')rmSync(source);
  else writeFileSync(source,JSON.stringify({toolName:'fixture-runner',arguments:{},output:'Error UPDATED_MARKER: previous source has changed and no longer proves the old advice.'})+'\n');
  const before=calls.length;
  const updated=await router.read(context,{ref:found.items[0].read_ref});
  assert.equal(updated.card.status,'stale');assert.ok(updated.card.version>first.card.version);
  assert.equal(updated.card.evidence[0].sourceAvailable,false);
  assert.equal((await a.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:first.card.id})).card.status,'stale');
  assert.equal((await a.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:dependent.card.id})).card.status,'withdrawn');
  assert.equal(calls.slice(before).some(call=>call.capability==='knowledge.ingest'),false);
  assert.ok(calls.slice(before).some(call=>call.deviceId==='B'&&call.capability==='knowledge.stat'));
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';

import { join } from 'node:path';

test('expected Palace domain miss preserves process availability for ingest and selected import',async t=>{
  const root=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'mcp-v12-palace-process-'));
  const logs=join(root,'logs'),dataRoot=join(root,'A');mkdirSync(logs);mkdirSync(dataRoot);
  const faults=[];
  const manifest={id:'palace',version:'1.0.12',dataSchemaVersion:1,isolation:'process',timeoutMs:10000,
    entry:fileURLToPath(new URL('../../dist/plugins/palace/plugin.js',import.meta.url)),
    capabilities:['knowledge.read','knowledge.ingest','knowledge.import','knowledge.search'].map(name=>({name,version:'1.0.0'}))};
  const driver=createPluginDriver(manifest,{deviceId:'A',dataRoot,sourceRoots:[logs],projectRoots:[],
    settings:{autoIndex:false,discoverKnownSources:false}},error=>faults.push(error));
  const origin=createPalaceProvider({deviceId:'B',dataRoot:join(root,'B'),sourceRoots:[logs],autoIndex:false});
  t.after(async()=>{await driver.stop();await origin.stop();rmSync(root,{recursive:true,force:true});});
  await driver.start();
  await assert.rejects(driver.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:'missing'}),error=>error.code==='KNOWLEDGE_NOT_FOUND');
  writeFileSync(join(logs,'events.log'),'Error PROCESS_IMPORT: retain the original source identity.\n');
  await driver.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});
  assert.equal((await driver.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:'PROCESS_IMPORT'})).items.length,1);
  await origin.start();await origin.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});
  const reference=(await origin.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:'PROCESS_IMPORT'})).items[0];
  const original=(await origin.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:reference.id})).card;
  await driver.invoke('knowledge.import',{card:original});
  const copied=(await driver.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:original.id})).card;
  assert.equal(copied.originDeviceId,'B');assert.equal(copied.servingDeviceId,'A');
  assert.equal(copied.contentHash,original.contentHash);assert.equal(faults.length,0);
});
