import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, appendFileSync, readFileSync, rmSync, cpSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { createPalaceProvider, cardHash } from '../../dist/plugins/palace/index.js';
import { LogPalace } from '../../dist/log-palace/store.js';
import { SourceCatalog } from '../../dist/plugins/palace/sources.js';

const sha=x=>createHash('sha256').update(x).digest('hex');
async function fixture(t,extra={}) {
  const root=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'mcp-v12-palace-'));
  const logs=join(root,'logs');mkdirSync(logs);
  const providers=[];
  const open=async(deviceId='A',options={})=>{
    const provider=createPalaceProvider({dataRoot:join(root,'data-'+deviceId),deviceId,sourceRoots:[logs],autoIndex:false,...extra,...options});
    await provider.start();providers.push(provider);return provider;
  };
  t.after(async()=>{for(const p of providers)await p.stop();rmSync(root,{recursive:true,force:true});});
  return {root,logs,open};
}
const search=(p,query,more={})=>p.invoke('knowledge.search',{query,...more});
async function seed(p,logs,text='Error SQLITE_BUSY: configure bounded retry; the attempt failed.') {
  writeFileSync(join(logs,'session.log'),text+'\n');
  await p.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});
  const results=await search(p,'SQLITE_BUSY');
  assert.equal(results.items.length,1);
  return (await p.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:results.items[0].read_ref})).card;
}
function tableCount(dataRoot,table) {
  const db=new DatabaseSync(join(dataRoot,'palace.sqlite'),{readOnly:true});
  try{return db.prepare('SELECT count(*) n FROM '+table).get().n;}finally{db.close();}
}

test('runtime roots preserve bounded known-source discovery; explicit isolated roots stay narrow',()=>{
  const dataRoot=join(tmpdir(),'mcp-v12-catalog-only'),runtimeLogs=join(dataRoot,'logs');
  const isolated=new SourceCatalog({dataRoot,deviceId:'fixture',sourceRoots:[runtimeLogs]});
  assert.deepEqual(isolated.sources,[runtimeLogs]);assert.equal(isolated.defaults,false);
  const configured=new SourceCatalog({dataRoot,deviceId:'fixture',sourceRoots:[runtimeLogs],discoverKnownSources:true});
  assert.ok(configured.sources.includes(runtimeLogs));assert.equal(configured.sources.length,5);assert.equal(configured.defaults,true);
});

test('P1 observation feed is bounded, read-only, resumable and does not claim verification',async t=>{
  const root=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'mcp-v12-p1feed-'));t.after(()=>rmSync(root,{recursive:true,force:true}));
  const path=join(root,'events.log');writeFileSync(path,'Error FIRST\nError SECOND\nError THIRD\n');
  const original=sha(readFileSync(path)),logs=new LogPalace(join(root,'index'));
  try{
    logs.add(path);logs.index({maxFiles:8,maxBytes:32768});
    const before=logs.sources(),seen=new Set();let cursor;
    do{
      const page=logs.observations({cursor,limit:1,maxBytes:2048});
      assert.ok(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(page))<=2048);
      for(const row of page.items){
        assert.match(row.hash,/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);assert.equal(row.source_available,true);
        assert.equal(row.verified,undefined);assert.ok(!seen.has(row.ref));seen.add(row.ref);
        assert.match(logs.read(row.ref).text,/Error/);
      }
      cursor=page.nextCursor;
    }while(cursor);
    assert.equal(seen.size,3);assert.deepEqual(logs.sources(),before);assert.equal(sha(readFileSync(path)),original);
  }finally{logs.close();}
});

test('offline cards survive restart; append is incremental and incomplete tails wait',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t),p=await f.open();
  const path=join(f.logs,'events.log');
  writeFileSync(path,'Error FIRST: missing configuration.\nError SECOND: waiting for complete source');
  const initial=sha(readFileSync(path));
  await p.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});
  assert.equal((await search(p,'FIRST')).items.length,1);
  assert.equal((await search(p,'SECOND')).items.length,0);
  assert.equal(sha(readFileSync(path)),initial);
  const before=tableCount(join(f.root,'data-A'),'cards');
  await p.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});
  assert.equal(tableCount(join(f.root,'data-A'),'cards'),before);
  appendFileSync(path,'\n');
  await p.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});
  const found=await search(p,'SECOND');assert.equal(found.items.length,1);
  const selected=(await p.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:found.items[0].id,evidence:true})).card;
  assert.equal(selected.status,'hypothesis');assert.equal(selected.contentHash,cardHash(selected));
  await p.stop();
  const restarted=await f.open();
  const read=await restarted.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:selected.id,evidence:true});
  assert.equal(read.card.id,selected.id);assert.equal(read.card.version,selected.version);
  assert.equal(read.evidence[0].verifiedBytes,true);
});

test('source rewrite, deletion and invalid UTF-8 are never presented as current verified source',async t=>{

  await p.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});
  assert.equal((await search(p,'SQLITE_BUSY')).items.length,0);
  assert.equal((await p.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:card.id})).card.status,'stale');

  rmSync(path);
  await p.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});
  assert.equal((await p.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:next.id})).card.status,'stale');
  writeFileSync(path,Buffer.concat([Buffer.from('Error INVALID_UTF8 '),Buffer.from([255]),Buffer.from('\n')]));
  await p.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});
  assert.equal((await search(p,'INVALID_UTF8')).items.length,0);

test('normalized duplicate observations consolidate without crossing project scope or inflating trust',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t),alpha=join(f.root,'alpha'),beta=join(f.root,'beta');
  for(const project of [alpha,beta])mkdirSync(join(project,'.mcp-palace'),{recursive:true});

  writeFileSync(second,'Error CONNECTION_RESET: retry after reconnecting.\n');

  await p.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});

  assert.equal(a.items.length,1);assert.equal(b.items.length,1);assert.notEqual(a.items[0].id,b.items[0].id);
  const card=(await p.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:a.items[0].id,projectId:'alpha'})).card;

  assert.equal(card.status,'hypothesis');assert.equal((await p.invoke('knowledge.skills',{projectId:'alpha'})).items.length,0);
  await p.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});
  assert.equal((await p.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:card.id})).card.version,card.version);
});

test('search/list neither import nor write cards; metadata and full read have hard budgets',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t),p=await f.open(),card=await seed(p,f.logs,'Error SQLITE_BUSY: '+('large output evidence '.repeat(600)));

  await p.invoke('knowledge.list',{});

test('B -> A -> C -> A imports preserve original identity and cannot create corroboration',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t),b=await f.open('B'),a=await f.open('A',{sourceRoots:[]}),c=await f.open('C',{sourceRoots:[]});
  const original=await seed(b,f.logs);
  assert.equal((await search(a,'SQLITE_BUSY')).items.length,0);
  await a.invoke('knowledge.import',{card:original});
  const aCopy=(await a.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:original.id})).card;
  await c.invoke('knowledge.import',{card:aCopy});
  const cCopy=(await c.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:original.id})).card;
  const back=await a.invoke('knowledge.import',{card:cCopy});
  assert.equal(back.deduplicated,true);assert.equal(back.imported,false);
  assert.equal(back.card.originDeviceId,'B');assert.equal(back.card.servingDeviceId,'A');
  assert.equal(back.card.version,original.version);assert.equal(back.card.contentHash,original.contentHash);
  assert.deepEqual(back.card.evidence.map(e=>e.id),original.evidence.map(e=>e.id));
  assert.equal(tableCount(join(f.root,'data-A'),'cards'),1);
  const tampered={...original,body:'forged body'};
  await assert.rejects(a.invoke('knowledge.import',{card:tampered}),/HASH_MISMATCH/);
});

test('automatic ingestion proposes a recurring explicit fix without trusting the source verdict',async t=>{

test('fake refs and caller success claims cannot activate a Skill',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t),p=await f.open(),source=await seed(p,f.logs);
  await assert.rejects(p.invoke('knowledge.learn',{summary:'Fake success',steps:['Never executed'],evidenceRefs:['fake:one','fake:two'],
    outcome:'success',exitCode:0,promoteImmediately:true}),/EVIDENCE_NOT_FOUND/);
  const learned=await p.invoke('knowledge.learn',{summary:'Bounded retry candidate',steps:['Configure bounded retries'],evidenceRefs:[source.id],
    outcome:'success',exitCode:0,validation:{passed:true},promoteImmediately:true});
  assert.equal(learned.activated,false);assert.equal(learned.card.status,'hypothesis');
  assert.equal(learned.reason,'VALIDATION_POLICY_NOT_CONFIGURED');
  assert.equal((await p.invoke('knowledge.skills',{})).items.length,0);
  const candidates=await p.invoke('knowledge.skills',{includeCandidates:true});

});

test('configured validator executes a real fixture and postcondition; corrections withdraw transitively',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t),outputPath=join(f.root,'verified-output.txt');
  let calls=0;
  const validationRunner=async()=>{
    calls++;
    const run=spawnSync(process.execPath,['-e','require("fs").writeFileSync(process.argv[1],"verified fixture result")',outputPath],{encoding:'utf8',timeout:5000});
    return {executionId:'fixture-'+calls,exitCode:run.status??99,postconditionPassed:existsSync(outputPath)&&readFileSync(outputPath,'utf8')==='verified fixture result',
      output:run.stdout+run.stderr,validatorVersion:'fixture/1'};
  };
  const p=await f.open('A',{validationRunner}),source=await seed(p,f.logs);
  const first=await p.invoke('knowledge.learn',{key:'retry',summary:'Validated retry',steps:['Run the fixture repair'],evidenceRefs:[source.id]});
  assert.equal(first.activated,true);assert.equal(first.card.status,'validated');assert.equal(calls,1);
  const second=await p.invoke('knowledge.learn',{key:'derived',summary:'Derived workflow',steps:['Follow tested retry'],evidenceRefs:[first.card.id]});
  assert.equal(second.activated,true);assert.equal((await p.invoke('knowledge.skills',{})).items.length,2);
  const selected=await p.invoke('knowledge.skills',{action:'read',ref:first.card.id});
  assert.match(selected.body,/fixture repair/);assert.equal(selected.active,true);
  const corrected=await p.invoke('knowledge.correct',{ref:source.id,body:'The retry claim was wrong for this version.',reason:'Fixture correction'});
  assert.ok(corrected.invalidated.includes(first.card.id));assert.ok(corrected.invalidated.includes(second.card.id));
  assert.equal((await p.invoke('knowledge.skills',{})).items.length,0);
  assert.equal((await p.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:second.card.id})).card.status,'withdrawn');
  await assert.rejects(p.invoke('knowledge.skills',{action:'read',ref:first.card.id}),/SKILL_NOT_ACTIVE/);
  await assert.rejects(p.invoke('knowledge.skills',{action:'rollback',ref:first.card.id,version:first.card.version}),/CORRECTED_EVIDENCE/);
  assert.ok(tableCount(join(f.root,'data-A'),'versions')>tableCount(join(f.root,'data-A'),'cards'));
});

test('exit zero alone and a nonzero exit with claimed success both fail configured validation',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t);
  const p=await f.open('A',{validationRunner:async()=>{const run=spawnSync(process.execPath,['-e','process.exit(0)'],{encoding:'utf8'});
    return {executionId:'zero-without-postcondition',exitCode:run.status,postconditionPassed:false,output:'claimed success',validatorVersion:'fixture/1'};}});
  const source=await seed(p,f.logs);
  const zero=await p.invoke('knowledge.learn',{summary:'No postcondition',steps:['Unchecked task'],evidenceRefs:[source.id]});
  assert.equal(zero.activated,false);assert.equal(zero.reason,'VALIDATION_FAILED');
  const q=await f.open('B',{validationRunner:async()=>{const run=spawnSync(process.execPath,['-e','process.stdout.write("success");process.exit(99)'],{encoding:'utf8'});
    return {executionId:'failed-with-claim',exitCode:run.status,postconditionPassed:true,output:run.stdout,validatorVersion:'fixture/1'};}});
  await q.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});const qSource=(await search(q,'SQLITE_BUSY')).items[0];
  const failed=await q.invoke('knowledge.learn',{summary:'False success',steps:['Failed fixture'],evidenceRefs:[qSource.id]});
  assert.equal(failed.activated,false);assert.equal((await q.invoke('knowledge.skills',{})).items.length,0);
});

  await restored.stop();

===== BEGIN SOURCE test/v12/palace-task-replay.test.js | original-bytes=7533 | sha256=38252cdea5d9765bbd562ca665ca03a56fd302f40c23551529cd9d7ee884c771 =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync, existsSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { performance } from 'node:perf_hooks';
import { createPalaceProvider } from '../../dist/plugins/palace/index.js';

test('deterministic executable task replay compares no-Palace retries with a validated reusable procedure',async t=>{
  const root=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),'mcp-v12-palace-replay-')),logs=join(root,'logs');
  mkdirSync(logs);
  const executable=join(root,'settlement.cjs'),ledger=join(root,'ledger.json');
  writeFileSync(executable,[
    "const fs=require('node:fs');",
    "const [ledger,key,timeout]=process.argv.slice(2);",
    "if(Number(timeout)<250){console.log(JSON.stringify({ok:false,error:'SQLITE_BUSY',operation:'settlement',retryable:true}));process.exit(75);}",
    "const entries=fs.existsSync(ledger)?JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(ledger,'utf8')):{};",
    "entries[key]=entries[key]||{chargeCount:1,key};",
    "fs.writeFileSync(ledger,JSON.stringify(entries));",
    "console.log(JSON.stringify({ok:true,key,chargeCount:entries[key].chargeCount}));"
  ].join('\n'));
  const execute=(key,timeout)=>{
    const result=spawnSync(process.execPath,[executable,ledger,key,String(timeout)],{encoding:'utf8',timeout:5000});
    const state=existsSync(ledger)?JSON.parse(readFileSync(ledger,'utf8')):{};
    return {exitCode:result.status??99,stdout:result.stdout,stderr:result.stderr,
      postcondition:result.status===0&&state[key]?.chargeCount===1&&state[key]?.key===key};
  };
  // Prior source claims are separate from held-out task IDs. A trusted runner must execute
  // the proposed recipe and inspect the resulting state before it becomes an active Skill.
  const observationA='Error SQLITE_BUSY during settlement. Fix: Set busyTimeoutMs = 250 and preserve the original idempotency key before retry.';
  const observationB='Resolved: independent reconnect trace completed settlement with busyTimeoutMs = 250 while the original idempotency key was reused; duplicate charge count stayed at one.';
  const contradictory='Rejected workaround: busyTimeoutMs = 0 repeated SQLITE_BUSY and did not complete the settlement.';
  writeFileSync(join(logs,'prior-a.log'),observationA+'\n');
  writeFileSync(join(logs,'prior-b.log'),observationB+'\n');
  writeFileSync(join(logs,'contradictory.log'),contradictory+'\n');
  assert.equal(execute('negative-zero-timeout',0).postcondition,false);
  let validationCalls=0;
  const provider=createPalaceProvider({deviceId:'replay',dataRoot:join(root,'state'),sourceRoots:[logs],autoIndex:true,
    validationRunner:async card=>{
      validationCalls++;
      const timeout=Number(card.body.match(/busyTimeoutMs\s*=\s*(\d+)/)?.[1]??0);
      const result=execute('training-validation-'+validationCalls,timeout);
      return {executionId:'replay-validation-'+validationCalls,exitCode:result.exitCode,postconditionPassed:result.postcondition,
        output:result.stdout+result.stderr,validatorVersion:'settlement-fixture/1'};
    }});
  t.after(async()=>{await provider.stop();rmSync(root,{recursive:true,force:true});});
  await provider.start();
  await provider.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{maxFiles:8,maxRecords:64,maxBytes:1024*1024});
  // Ingestion may propose a hypothesis candidate, but raw log text never reaches the execution
  // validator or becomes an active Skill automatically.
  const candidates=await provider.invoke('knowledge.skills',{includeCandidates:true});
  assert.equal(validationCalls,0,'ingest must never execute the validation runner implicitly');
  const observations=await provider.invoke('knowledge.list',{limit:32,maxBytes:16384});
  const procedureIds=new Set(candidates.items.map(item=>item.id));
  const evidence=observations.items.filter(item=>!procedureIds.has(item.id)&&item.summary.includes('250')&&!item.summary.includes('Rejected'));
  assert.ok(evidence.length>=2,'validated procedure requires at least two independent positive source traces');
  const promoted=await provider.invoke('knowledge.learn',{key:'explicit-settlement-retry',summary:'Validated settlement retry procedure',
    steps:['Set busyTimeoutMs = 250 and preserve the original idempotency key before retry.'],evidenceRefs:evidence.slice(0,2).map(item=>item.id)});
  assert.equal(promoted.activated,true);assert.equal(validationCalls,1);
  const active=await provider.invoke('knowledge.skills',{});
  assert.equal(active.items.length,1);
  const tasks=['held-out-a','held-out-b','held-out-c'];
  const baseline={taskSuccess:0,repeatedErrors:0,executionCalls:0,retrievalCalls:0,agentVisibleBytes:0,elapsedMs:0};
  let began=performance.now();
  for(const task of tasks){
    let previousError=false;
    // Fixed baseline policy: retry default once, then use its diagnostic fallback.
    for(const timeout of [0,0,250]){
      const result=execute('baseline-'+task,timeout);baseline.executionCalls++;
      baseline.agentVisibleBytes+=Buffer.byteLength(result.stdout+result.stderr);
      if(result.exitCode!==0){if(previousError)baseline.repeatedErrors++;previousError=true;}
      if(result.postcondition){baseline.taskSuccess++;break;}
    }
  }
  baseline.elapsedMs=performance.now()-began;
  const palace={taskSuccess:0,repeatedErrors:0,executionCalls:0,retrievalCalls:0,agentVisibleBytes:0,elapsedMs:0};
  began=performance.now();
  const found=await provider.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:'retry idempotency',limit:3,maxBytes:4096});palace.retrievalCalls++;
  palace.agentVisibleBytes+=Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(found));
  const selected=found.items.find(item=>item.status==='validated');assert.ok(selected);
  const read=await provider.invoke('knowledge.skills',{action:'read',ref:selected.id,maxBytes:8192});palace.retrievalCalls++;
  palace.agentVisibleBytes+=Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(read));
  const remembered=Number(read.body.match(/busyTimeoutMs\s*=\s*(\d+)/)?.[1]??0);
  for(const task of tasks){
    const result=execute('palace-'+task,remembered);palace.executionCalls++;
    palace.agentVisibleBytes+=Buffer.byteLength(result.stdout+result.stderr);
    if(result.postcondition)palace.taskSuccess++;
  }
  palace.elapsedMs=performance.now()-began;
  const result={benchmark:'palace-executable-replay-v1',node:process.version,tasks:tasks.length,validationCalls,
    taskPrompt:'Complete each new settlement after a reconnect while preserving its supplied idempotency key.',
    baseline,palace,
    limits:['Deterministic fixture policies; no LLM productivity or general task-success claim.',
      'Baseline retries once before its fixed diagnostic fallback; Palace reuses one selected validated recipe across three new task IDs.',
      'All tasks execute a real child process and inspect ledger postconditions.',
      'Agent-visible bytes include retrieval metadata/evidence and may increase despite fewer repeated errors.',
      'Training/validation cost is reported separately and is not hidden in the replay improvement.']};
  console.log('PALACE_TASK_REPLAY '+JSON.stringify(result));
  if(process.env.MCP_V12_PALACE_REPLAY_OUTPUT)writeFileSync(process.env.MCP_V12_PALACE_REPLAY_OUTPUT,JSON.stringify(result,null,2)+'\n');
  assert.equal(baseline.taskSuccess,3);assert.equal(palace.taskSuccess,3);
  assert.equal(baseline.repeatedErrors,3);assert.equal(palace.repeatedErrors,0);
  assert.equal(palace.executionCalls,3);
});

import test from 'node:test';

const exec = promisify(execFile);

async function rootFor(t) { const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'v12-cli-')); t.after(() => fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true })); return root; }
test('runtime ownership rejects a concurrent owner and releases only its own lease', async t => {
  const root = await rootFor(t), release = await acquireServerLease(root);
  await assert.rejects(acquireServerLease(root), { code: 'RUNTIME_ALREADY_RUNNING' });
  await release(); const second = await acquireServerLease(root); await second();
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(root, 'server-runtime.lock'), '{broken');
  await assert.rejects(acquireServerLease(root), { code: 'RUNTIME_LOCK_INVALID' });
});

test('maintenance lease detects Device-only ownership and refuses unresponsive legacy control', async t => {
  const root = await rootFor(t), paths = { root, identity: path.join(root, 'gateway-identity.json'), config: path.join(root, 'gateway-config.json'), status: path.join(root, 'gateway-status.json'), runtime: path.join(root, 'runtime'), update: path.join(root, 'update') };
  let stopAttempts = 0;
  const owner = new RuntimeOwner({ paths, mode: 'foreground', onStop: async () => { stopAttempts++; throw new Error('fixture cleanup not proven'); } });
  await owner.acquire();
  try {
    await assert.rejects(acquireServerLease(root), { code: 'RUNTIME_ALREADY_RUNNING' });
    await assert.rejects(fs.stat(path.join(root, 'server-runtime.lock')), { code: 'ENOENT' });
    const release = await acquireServerLease(root, { allowDeviceOwnerPid: process.pid });
    await assert.rejects(acquireServerLease(root), { code: 'RUNTIME_ALREADY_RUNNING' });
    await release();
    assert.equal(await stopRuntimeOwner(paths), true, 'stop acknowledges the request');
    assert.equal((await probeRuntimeOwner(paths)).pid, process.pid, 'failed cleanup retains the actual owner');
    assert.equal(stopAttempts, 1);
    const contender = new RuntimeOwner({ paths, mode: 'foreground' });
    await assert.rejects(acquireRuntimeOwner(contender, { confirmTakeover: () => true, waitMs: 40, pollMs: 5 }), /did not release runtime ownership/);
    assert.equal((await probeRuntimeOwner(paths)).pid, process.pid);
  } finally { await owner.release(); }
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(root, 'runtime-control.json'), '{}');
  await assert.rejects(acquireServerLease(root), { code: 'DEVICE_RUNTIME_STOP_NOT_PROVEN' });
  await assert.rejects(fs.stat(path.join(root, 'server-runtime.lock')), { code: 'ENOENT' });
});

  const invoke = async (...args) => JSON.parse((await exec(process.execPath, [entry, 'plugins', '--runtime-dir', root, ...args])).stdout);
  assert.equal((await invoke('install', manifest, '--enable')).plugins[0].enabled, true);
  assert.equal((await invoke('disable', 'cli-reference')).plugins[0].enabled, false);
  assert.equal((await invoke('uninstall', 'cli-reference')).plugins[0].installed, false);
  assert.equal((await invoke('list')).plugins[0].state, 'uninstalled');

test('plugin maintenance retains ownership until cleanup is proven, including failed upgrades', async t => {
  const root = await rootFor(t), entry = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../dist/mcp-device.js', import.meta.url));
  const hostUrl = new URL('../../dist/platform/plugin-host.js', import.meta.url).href;
  const manifest = path.join(root, 'manifest.json');
  await fs.writeFile(manifest, JSON.stringify({ id: 'lease-fixture', entry: 'unused.mjs' }));
  for (const action of ['upgrade', 'purge']) {
    for (const cleanupFails of [true, false]) {
      const state = path.join(root, action + '-' + cleanupFails), lock = path.join(state, 'server-runtime.lock');
      const marker = path.join(root, action + '-' + cleanupFails + '.json'), preload = marker + '.mjs';
      await fs.writeFile(preload, `import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import { PluginHost } from ${JSON.stringify(hostUrl)};
PluginHost.prototype[${JSON.stringify(action)}] = async function() { throw Object.assign(new Error('fixture operation failed'), { code: 'FIXTURE_OPERATION_FAILED' }); };
PluginHost.prototype.close = async function() {
  await fs.writeFile(${JSON.stringify(marker)}, await fs.readFile(${JSON.stringify(lock)}, 'utf8'));
  if (${cleanupFails}) throw Object.assign(new Error('fixture cleanup not proven'), { code: 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED' });
};\n`);
      await assert.rejects(exec(process.execPath, ['--import', pathToFileURL(preload).href, entry, 'plugins', '--runtime-dir', state, action, action === 'upgrade' ? manifest : 'lease-fixture']), error => {
        assert.equal(error.code, 1);
        assert.match(error.stderr, cleanupFails ? /PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED/ : /FIXTURE_OPERATION_FAILED/);
        assert.doesNotMatch(error.stdout, /"ok": true/);
        return true;
      });
      assert.ok(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(marker, 'utf8')).pid > 0, 'maintenance still owns the lease while closing');
      if (cleanupFails) assert.ok(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(lock, 'utf8')).pid > 0, 'unproven cleanup retains the lease');
      else await assert.rejects(fs.stat(lock), { code: 'ENOENT' });
    }
  }
});

test('public knowledge tools preserve signed references across restart and isolate concurrent caller contexts', async t => {
  const root = await rootFor(t), calls = [];
  const card = account => ({ id: 'card-' + account, version: 1, contentHash: 'hash-' + account, summary: 'A useful fact', body: 'Exact useful fact', status: 'observed', originDeviceId: 'provider-' + account, servingDeviceId: 'provider-' + account, scope: { visibility: 'account' }, evidence: [], derivedFrom: [], provenance: ['provider-' + account], updatedAt: 1, lastVerifiedAt: 1 });
  const context = account => ({ accountId: account, sessionId: 'session-' + account,
    listDevices: () => [{ deviceId: 'client-' + account, online: true, capabilities: [] }, { deviceId: 'provider-' + account, online: true, capabilities: ['knowledge.search', 'knowledge.read', 'knowledge.stat'] }],
    async callDevice({ deviceId, tool }) { calls.push([account, deviceId, tool]); await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 5)); assert.equal(deviceId, 'provider-' + account); const value = card(account); return tool === 'knowledge.search' ? { items: [value] } : tool === 'knowledge.stat' ? value : { card: value }; }
  });
  const tools = await createPlatformTools(root), search = tools.find(tool => tool.name === 'knowledge.search');
  const results = await Promise.all(['a', 'b'].map(account => search.handler({ device_id: 'client-' + account, query: 'fact' }, context(account))));
  assert.equal(results[0].items[0].id, 'card-a'); assert.equal(results[1].items[0].id, 'card-b');
  const restarted = await createPlatformTools(root), read = restarted.find(tool => tool.name === 'knowledge.read');
  const result = await read.handler({ device_id: 'client-a', ref: results[0].items[0].read_ref }, context('a'));
  assert.equal(result.card.id, 'card-a'); assert.equal(result.imported, false);
  await assert.rejects(read.handler({ device_id: 'client-b', ref: results[0].items[0].read_ref }, context('b')), { code: 'ACCESS_DENIED' });
  assert.equal(calls.some(call => call[2] === 'knowledge.import'), false);
  assert.equal(tools.some(tool => tool.name === 'knowledge.import'), false);
  const invoke = tools.find(tool => tool.name === 'capability.call');
  await assert.rejects(invoke.handler({ device_id: 'client-a', capability: 'knowledge.import', arguments: {} }, context('a')), { code: 'CAPABILITY_PRIVATE' });
  assert.deepEqual((await fs.readdir(root)).sort(), ['knowledge-reference.key']);
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';

test('bundled provider lifecycle and fatal plugin leave the actual shared Device Core functional', { timeout: 30000 }, async t => {
  const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'v12-platform-core-'));
  process.env.MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR = root;
  const { GatewayDeviceIdentity } = await import('../../dist/device/gateway-identity.js');
  const identity = new GatewayDeviceIdentity(path.join(root, 'gateway-identity.json'));
  const record = await identity.loadOrCreate();
  const setup = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: record.deviceId });
  for (const manifest of bundledPluginManifests()) await setup.install(manifest, { enabled: true, settings: { autoIndex: false, discoverKnownSources: false } });
  await setup.close();
  const { createPlatformDeviceCore } = await import('../../dist/platform/device-runtime.js');
  const core = await createPlatformDeviceCore({ deviceId: record.deviceId, stateRoot: root, allowedRoots: [root], sourceRoots: [], projectRoots: [] });
  t.after(async () => { await core.close(); await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); });
  assert.ok(core.capabilities.includes('knowledge.search'));
  assert.ok((await core.invoke('platform.capabilities')).contracts.some(capability => capability.name === 'knowledge.search' && capability.version === '1.0.0'));
  await assert.rejects(core.invoke('platform.invoke', { name: 'knowledge.search', version: '^2.0.0', input: { query: 'fixture' } }), { code: 'CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE' });
  const privateData = core.plugins.dataRoot('palace'), initialData = (await fs.readdir(privateData)).filter(name => !/-(?:wal|shm)$/.test(name));
  assert.ok(initialData.length > 0);
  const target = path.join(root, 'fixture.txt');
  const written = await core.invokeNative('write_file', { path: target, content: 'ordinary tools survive', mode: 'rewrite' });
  assert.notEqual(written.isError, true);
  const read = async () => { const result = await core.invokeNative('read_file', { path: target }); assert.notEqual(result.isError, true); assert.match(JSON.stringify(result), /ordinary tools survive/); };
  await core.plugins.disable('palace'); await read(); assert.equal(core.capabilities.includes('knowledge.search'), false);
  await core.plugins.enable('palace'); assert.ok(core.capabilities.includes('knowledge.search'));
  const faultEntry = path.join(root, 'fault.mjs');
  await fs.writeFile(faultEntry, `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke(n,i){if(i==='crash')process.exit(19);while(true){}}});`);
  await core.plugins.install({ id: 'fatal-fixture', version: '1.0.0', dataSchemaVersion: 1, isolation: 'process', entry: faultEntry, capabilities: [{ name: 'fault.execute', version: '1.0.0' }], timeoutMs: 1000 }, { enabled: true });
  const failed = assert.rejects(core.invoke('fault.execute', 'hang'), { code: 'PLUGIN_TIMEOUT' });
  await read(); await failed;
  await core.plugins.enable('fatal-fixture'); await assert.rejects(core.invoke('fault.execute', 'crash')); await read();
  await core.plugins.uninstall('palace'); await read();
  assert.equal(core.capabilities.includes('knowledge.search'), false);
  const retainedData = await fs.readdir(privateData);
  assert.ok(initialData.every(name => retainedData.includes(name)), 'Durable plugin files remain; SQLite may remove transient WAL/SHM when closed');
  assert.equal((await identity.loadOrCreate()).deviceId, record.deviceId);
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(path.join(root, 'gateway-identity.json'), 'utf8')).publicKeyPem, record.publicKeyPem);
  const closePlugins = core.plugins.close.bind(core.plugins);
  core.plugins.close = async () => { throw Object.assign(new Error('Fixture resource release refused'), { code: 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED' }); };
  try {
    await assert.rejects(core.close(), { code: 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED' });
    await assert.rejects(core.close(), { code: 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED' });
    assert.deepEqual(core.capabilities, []);
    await assert.rejects(core.invokeNative('read_file', { path: target }), { code: 'DEVICE_NOT_READY' });
  } finally { core.plugins.close = closePlugins; }
  await core.close();
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { PluginHost } from '../../dist/platform/plugin-host.js';

async function fixture(t) {
  const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-v12-bundle-'));
  const hosts = [];
  const create = () => { const host = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'bundle-fixture' }); hosts.push(host); return host; };
  t.after(async () => { for (const host of hosts.reverse()) await host.close(); await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true, maxRetries: 5, retryDelay: 50 }); });
  const write = async (name, body) => { const entry = path.join(root, name + '.mjs'); await fs.writeFile(entry, body); return entry; };
  const manifest = (entry, extra = {}) => ({ id: 'bundle', entry, version: '1.0.0', dataSchemaVersion: 1,
    capabilities: [{ name: 'bundle.value', version: '1.0.0' }], isolation: 'in-process', trusted: true, timeoutMs: 2000, ...extra });
  const registry = async () => JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(path.join(root, 'plugins', 'registry.json'), 'utf8'));
  return { root, create, host: create(), write, manifest, registry };
}
const retaining = "import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';export const createPlugin=c=>({async invoke(){return {value:await fs.readFile(path.join(c.dataRoot,'value'),'utf8'),setting:c.settings?.label}}});";
const simple = "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>true});";

test('bundled provenance permits entry-only relocation with identical contract and retained settings/data', async t => {
  const { host, create, write, manifest, registry } = await fixture(t);
  const oldEntry = await write('original', retaining), oldManifest = manifest(oldEntry);
  await host.ensureBundled([oldManifest]);
  await host.configure('bundle', { label: 'retained setting' });
  await fs.mkdir(host.dataRoot('bundle'), { recursive: true });
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(host.dataRoot('bundle'), 'value'), 'retained data');
  await host.start();
  assert.equal(host.list()[0].installationSource, 'distribution');
  assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('bundle.value', {}), { value: 'retained data', setting: 'retained setting' });
  await host.close(); await fs.unlink(oldEntry);
  const relocated = await write('relocated', retaining);
  // Property order is not a contract change.
  const moved = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries({ ...oldManifest, entry: relocated }).reverse());
  const restarted = create(); await restarted.ensureBundled([moved]);
  const beforeStart = (await registry()).plugins.bundle;
  assert.equal(beforeStart.manifest.entry, relocated); assert.equal(beforeStart.enabled, true);
  assert.equal(beforeStart.schemaVersion, 1); assert.equal(beforeStart.settings.label, 'retained setting');
  assert.equal(beforeStart.installationSource, 'distribution'); assert.equal(beforeStart.bundleUpgradeRequired, undefined);
  await restarted.start();
  assert.deepEqual(await restarted.invoke('bundle.value', {}), { value: 'retained data', setting: 'retained setting' });
});

test('disabled bundles relocate without activation and uninstall tombstones remain byte-for-byte unchanged', async t => {
  const { host, create, write, manifest, registry, root } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('disabled-old', simple);
  await host.ensureBundled([manifest(entry)]); await host.disable('bundle');
  await fs.mkdir(host.dataRoot('bundle'), { recursive: true }); await fs.writeFile(path.join(host.dataRoot('bundle'), 'marker'), 'keep');
  await host.close(); await fs.unlink(entry);
  const nextEntry = await write('disabled-new', simple), next = manifest(nextEntry);
  const restarted = create(); await restarted.ensureBundled([next]); await restarted.start();
  assert.deepEqual(restarted.capabilities(), []); assert.equal(restarted.list()[0].state, 'disabled');
  assert.equal(restarted.list()[0].manifest.entry, nextEntry); assert.equal(restarted.list()[0].enabled, false);
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(restarted.dataRoot('bundle'), 'marker'), 'utf8'), 'keep');
  await restarted.uninstall('bundle'); await restarted.close();
  const registryPath = path.join(root, 'plugins', 'registry.json'), bytes = await fs.readFile(registryPath, 'utf8');
  const third = create(); await third.ensureBundled([manifest(nextEntry, { version: '9.0.0', dataSchemaVersion: 9 })]); await third.start();
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(registryPath, 'utf8'), bytes);
  assert.equal((await registry()).plugins.bundle.installed, false); assert.deepEqual(third.capabilities(), []);
});

test('manual and unmarked records are never adopted or replaced by bundled manifests', async t => {
  const { host, create, write, manifest, root } = await fixture(t);
  const manualEntry = await write('manual', simple), replacement = await write('distribution', simple);
  await host.install(manifest(manualEntry), { enabled: false });
  const registryPath = path.join(root, 'plugins', 'registry.json');
  const original = await fs.readFile(registryPath, 'utf8');
  await host.ensureBundled([manifest(replacement)]);
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(registryPath, 'utf8'), original);
  assert.equal(host.list()[0].installationSource, 'manual');
  await host.close();
  const legacy = JSON.parse(original); delete legacy.plugins.bundle.installationSource;
  await fs.writeFile(registryPath, JSON.stringify(legacy));
  const unmarkedBytes = await fs.readFile(registryPath, 'utf8'), reopened = create();
  await reopened.ensureBundled([manifest(replacement)]);
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(registryPath, 'utf8'), unmarkedBytes);
  assert.equal(reopened.list()[0].manifest.entry, manualEntry);
  await reopened.uninstall('bundle'); await reopened.install(manifest(replacement));
  assert.equal(reopened.list()[0].installationSource, 'manual');
});

test('same-path distribution version change blocks execution until explicit upgrade migrates once', { timeout: 15000 }, async t => {
  const { host, create, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const v1 = "import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';export const createPlugin=c=>({async start(){const p=path.join(c.dataRoot,'value');try{await fs.access(p)}catch{await fs.writeFile(p,'one')}},invoke:()=>1});";
  const entry = await write('same-path', v1), m1 = manifest(entry, { isolation: 'process', trusted: false });
  await host.ensureBundled([m1]); await host.start(); assert.equal(await host.invoke('bundle.value', {}), 1); await host.close();
  const v2 = "import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';export async function migrate(o){await fs.appendFile(path.join(o.dataRoot,'migrations'),'m');await fs.writeFile(path.join(o.dataRoot,'value'),'two')}export const createPlugin=c=>({async start(){await fs.appendFile(path.join(c.dataRoot,'starts'),'s')},invoke:()=>2});";
  await fs.writeFile(entry, v2);
  const m2 = { ...m1, version: '2.0.0', dataSchemaVersion: 2 };
  const reopened = create(); await reopened.ensureBundled([m2]); await reopened.start();
  assert.deepEqual(reopened.capabilities(), []);
  assert.equal(reopened.list()[0].manifest.version, '1.0.0'); assert.equal(reopened.list()[0].schemaVersion, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(reopened.list()[0].bundleUpgradeRequired, { version: '2.0.0', dataSchemaVersion: 2 });
  await assert.rejects(reopened.enable('bundle'), { code: 'PLUGIN_UPGRADE_REQUIRED' });
  await assert.rejects(fs.access(path.join(reopened.dataRoot('bundle'), 'starts')), { code: 'ENOENT' });
  await assert.rejects(fs.access(path.join(reopened.dataRoot('bundle'), 'migrations')), { code: 'ENOENT' });
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(reopened.dataRoot('bundle'), 'value'), 'utf8'), 'one');
  await reopened.close();
  const restarted = create(); await restarted.start(); // Gate also survives a host that does not call ensureBundled.
  await assert.rejects(restarted.enable('bundle'), { code: 'PLUGIN_UPGRADE_REQUIRED' });
  assert.deepEqual(restarted.capabilities(), []);
  const failedMigration = "import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';export async function migrate(o){await fs.writeFile(path.join(o.dataRoot,'value'),'corrupted');throw new Error('migration rejected')}export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>99});";
  await fs.writeFile(entry, failedMigration);
  await assert.rejects(restarted.upgrade(m2), /migration rejected/);
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(restarted.dataRoot('bundle'), 'value'), 'utf8'), 'one');
  assert.equal(restarted.list()[0].bundleUpgradeRequired.version, '2.0.0');
  await assert.rejects(restarted.enable('bundle'), { code: 'PLUGIN_UPGRADE_REQUIRED' });
  await fs.writeFile(entry, v2); await restarted.upgrade(m2);
  assert.equal(await restarted.invoke('bundle.value', {}), 2);
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(restarted.dataRoot('bundle'), 'migrations'), 'utf8'), 'm');
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(restarted.dataRoot('bundle'), 'starts'), 'utf8'), 's');
  assert.equal(restarted.list()[0].bundleUpgradeRequired, undefined);
  assert.equal(restarted.list()[0].installationSource, 'distribution');
  await restarted.ensureBundled([m2]);
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(restarted.dataRoot('bundle'), 'migrations'), 'utf8'), 'm');
});

test('non-entry contract changes also gate disabled bundles without changing user enablement', async t => {
  const { host, create, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('contract', simple), m = manifest(entry);
  await host.ensureBundled([m]); await host.disable('bundle'); await host.close();
  const reopened = create(); await reopened.ensureBundled([{ ...m, timeoutMs: 9000 }]); await reopened.start();
  assert.equal(reopened.list()[0].enabled, false); assert.equal(reopened.list()[0].state, 'disabled');
  assert.equal(reopened.list()[0].manifest.timeoutMs, 2000);
  await assert.rejects(reopened.enable('bundle'), { code: 'PLUGIN_UPGRADE_REQUIRED' });
  assert.equal(reopened.list()[0].enabled, false); assert.deepEqual(reopened.capabilities(), []);
});

test('entry relocation recovers only a missing-entry fault and leaves unrelated faults explicit', async t => {
  const { host, create, write, manifest, root } = await fixture(t);
  const missing = path.join(root, 'missing.mjs');
  await host.ensureBundled([manifest(missing)]); await host.start();
  assert.equal(host.list()[0].state, 'faulted'); assert.match(host.list()[0].error, /^ENOENT:/); await host.close();
  const relocated = await write('present', simple), reopened = create();
  await reopened.ensureBundled([manifest(relocated)]); await reopened.start();
  assert.equal(await reopened.invoke('bundle.value', {}), true); await reopened.close();
  const registryPath = path.join(root, 'plugins', 'registry.json'), registry = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(registryPath, 'utf8'));
  registry.plugins.bundle.state = 'faulted'; registry.plugins.bundle.error = 'PLUGIN_FAULT: observed plugin failure';
  await fs.writeFile(registryPath, JSON.stringify(registry)); await fs.unlink(relocated);
  const next = await write('present-again', simple), third = create();
  await third.ensureBundled([manifest(next)]); await third.start();
  assert.equal(third.list()[0].state, 'faulted'); assert.deepEqual(third.capabilities(), []);
});

async function fixture(t) {

  const host = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'fixture-device' });
  t.after(async () => { await host.close(); await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); });
  const write = async (name, body) => { const entry = path.join(root, name + '.mjs'); await fs.writeFile(entry, body); return entry; };
  const manifest = (id, entry, caps = [{ name: 'text.echo', version: '1.0.0' }], extra = {}) => ({ id, version: '1.0.0', dataSchemaVersion: 1, isolation: 'in-process', trusted: true, entry, capabilities: caps, timeoutMs: 1000, ...extra });
  return { root, host, write, manifest };

test('unrelated plugin lifecycle preserves data and Core-owned state; reinstall resumes', async t => {
  const { root, host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const coreMarker = path.join(root, 'identity-marker'); await fs.writeFile(coreMarker, 'do not own this');
  const entry = await write('echo', `import fs from 'node:fs/promises'; import path from 'node:path'; export function createPlugin(c){return {async start(){await fs.writeFile(path.join(c.dataRoot,'retained.txt'),'retained')},invoke(n,i){return {echo:i,device:c.deviceId}}}}`);
  const m = manifest('reference', entry); await host.install(m, { enabled: true }); await host.start();
  assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('text.echo', { text: 'hello' }), { echo: { text: 'hello' }, device: 'fixture-device' });
  await host.disable('reference'); assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []);
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(host.dataRoot('reference'), 'retained.txt'), 'utf8'), 'retained');
  await host.enable('reference'); assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), ['text.echo']);
  await host.uninstall('reference'); assert.equal(await fs.readFile(coreMarker, 'utf8'), 'do not own this');
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(host.dataRoot('reference'), 'retained.txt'), 'utf8'), 'retained');
  await host.install(m, { enabled: true }); assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('text.echo', 42), { echo: 42, device: 'fixture-device' });
  await host.disable('reference'); await host.purge('reference'); await assert.rejects(fs.stat(host.dataRoot('reference')), { code: 'ENOENT' });
});

test('capability dependencies, priority, augmentation and graceful loss are generic', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const base = await write('base', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:(n,i)=>({value:i})});`);
  const augment = await write('augment', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:(n,{result})=>({...result,augmented:true})});`);
  await host.install(manifest('base', base, [{ name: 'text.echo', version: '1.0.0', priority: 10 }]), { enabled: true });
  await host.install(manifest('augment', augment, [{ name: 'text.echo', version: '1.0.0', role: 'augmenter' }], { requires: [{ name: 'text.echo', version: '^1.0.0' }] }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start(); assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('text.echo', 'x'), { value: 'x', augmented: true });
  await host.disable('base'); assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []); assert.equal(host.list().find(p => p.manifest.id === 'augment').state, 'faulted');
});

test('missing dependencies and cycles fail without bringing down the host', async t => {

  await host.start(); assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []); assert.ok(host.list().every(p => p.state === 'faulted'));

test('process crash and synchronous hang are isolated and bounded', { timeout: 15000 }, async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('fatal', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke(n,i){if(i==='crash')process.exit(19);while(true){}}});`);
  await host.install(manifest('fatal', entry, [{ name: 'fault.run', version: '1.0.0' }], { isolation: 'process', trusted: false, timeoutMs: 1000 }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start(); await assert.rejects(host.invoke('fault.run', 'crash'));
  await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100)); assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []);
  await host.enable('fatal'); const start = Date.now(); await assert.rejects(host.invoke('fault.run', 'hang'), error => error.code === 'PLUGIN_TIMEOUT');
  assert.ok(Date.now() - start < 5000); await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
  assert.equal(host.list()[0].state, 'faulted'); assert.equal(2 + 2, 4);
});

test('cooperative cancellation acknowledges the original request and keeps the process provider healthy', { timeout: 10000 }, async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('cooperative-cancel', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke(n,i,c){if(i!=='wait')return 'ok';return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const timer=setTimeout(()=>resolve('late'),5000);c.signal.addEventListener('abort',()=>{clearTimeout(timer);reject(Object.assign(new Error('cancelled'),{code:'CANCELLED'}))},{once:true})})}});`);
  await host.install(manifest('cooperative-cancel', entry, [{ name: 'cancel.run', version: '1.0.0' }], { isolation: 'process', trusted: false, timeoutMs: 3000 }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start();
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const pending = host.invoke('cancel.run', 'wait', { signal: controller.signal });
  setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 50);
  await assert.rejects(pending, { code: 'CANCELLED' });
  await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 150));
  assert.equal(host.list()[0].state, 'enabled');
  assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), ['cancel.run']);
  assert.equal(await host.invoke('cancel.run', 'next'), 'ok');
});

test('ordinary domain errors preserve a cooperative provider; thrown exceptions fault only that plugin', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('errors', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke(n,i){if(i==='invalid')throw Object.assign(new Error('input'),{code:'INVALID_ARGUMENT'});if(i==='throw')throw new Error('bug');return 'ok'}});`);
  await host.install(manifest('errors', entry), { enabled: true }); await host.start();

});

test('explicit upgrade migrates private data and failed migration rolls back', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const v1 = await write('v1', `import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';export const createPlugin=c=>({async start(){try{await fs.access(path.join(c.dataRoot,'value'))}catch{await fs.writeFile(path.join(c.dataRoot,'value'),'one')}},invoke:()=>1});`);
  await host.install(manifest('versioned', v1), { enabled: true }); await host.start();
  const v2 = await write('v2', `import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';export async function migrate(o){await fs.writeFile(path.join(o.dataRoot,'value'),'two')}export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>2});`);
  await host.upgrade(manifest('versioned', v2, undefined, { version: '2.0.0', dataSchemaVersion: 2 }));
  assert.equal(await host.invoke('text.echo', {}), 2); assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(host.dataRoot('versioned'), 'value'), 'utf8'), 'two');
  const bad = await write('bad', `import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';export async function migrate(o){await fs.writeFile(path.join(o.dataRoot,'value'),'corrupted');throw new Error('migration fail')}export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>3});`);
  await assert.rejects(host.upgrade(manifest('versioned', bad, undefined, { version: '3.0.0', dataSchemaVersion: 3 })));
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(host.dataRoot('versioned'), 'value'), 'utf8'), 'two'); assert.equal(await host.invoke('text.echo', {}), 2);
});

test('upgrade intent recovery restores or commits complete generations after process loss', async t => {
  async function scenario(phase) {
    const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-v12-upgrade-recovery-'));
    const entry1 = path.join(root, 'v1.mjs'), entry2 = path.join(root, 'v2.mjs');
    await fs.writeFile(entry1, `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>1});`);
    await fs.writeFile(entry2, `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>2});`);
    const manifest = (entry, version) => ({ id: 'recoverable', version, dataSchemaVersion: 1, isolation: 'in-process', trusted: true, entry, capabilities: [{ name: 'recover.value', version: '1.0.0' }], timeoutMs: 1000 });
    const first = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'fixture' });
    await first.install(manifest(entry1, '1.0.0'), { enabled: true });
    await fs.mkdir(first.dataRoot('recoverable'), { recursive: true });
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(first.dataRoot('recoverable'), 'value'), 'old');
    await first.close();
    const registryPath = path.join(root, 'plugins', 'registry.json');
    const registry = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(registryPath, 'utf8'));
    const previous = structuredClone(registry.plugins.recoverable), next = structuredClone(previous);
    next.manifest = manifest(entry2, '2.0.0'); next.state = 'enabled';
    const token = randomUUID(), pluginRoot = path.join(root, 'plugins', 'recoverable');
    const candidateName = 'upgrade-candidate-' + token, backupName = 'upgrade-backup-' + token, failedName = 'upgrade-failed-' + token;
    const data = path.join(pluginRoot, 'data'), candidate = path.join(pluginRoot, candidateName), backup = path.join(pluginRoot, backupName);
    await fs.cp(data, candidate, { recursive: true }); await fs.writeFile(path.join(candidate, 'value'), 'new');
    if (phase !== 'pre-swap') {
      await fs.rename(data, backup); if (phase !== 'swapping') await fs.rename(candidate, data);
    }
    if (phase === 'swapped' || phase === 'committed') {
      registry.revision += 1; registry.plugins.recoverable = next; await fs.writeFile(registryPath, JSON.stringify(registry, null, 2) + '\n');
    }
    const intent = { version: 1, id: 'recoverable', token, pid: 2147483646, startedAt: Date.now(),
      phase: phase === 'pre-swap' ? 'candidate-ready' : phase, candidate: candidateName, backup: backupName, failed: failedName, previous, next };
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(pluginRoot, 'upgrade-intent.json'), JSON.stringify(intent, null, 2) + '\n');
    const recovered = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'fixture' }); await recovered.load();
    const expected = phase === 'committed' ? 'new' : 'old';
    assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(recovered.dataRoot('recoverable'), 'value'), 'utf8'), expected);
    assert.equal(recovered.list()[0].manifest.version, phase === 'committed' ? '2.0.0' : '1.0.0');
    for (const name of ['upgrade-intent.json', candidateName, backupName, failedName]) await assert.rejects(fs.lstat(path.join(pluginRoot, name)), { code: 'ENOENT' });
    await recovered.close(); await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true, maxRetries: 5, retryDelay: 50 });
  }
  await scenario('pre-swap'); await scenario('swapping'); await scenario('swapped'); await scenario('committed');
});

test('external lifecycle changes remove dependent capability advertisements without restart', { timeout: 10000 }, async t => {
  const { root, host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('external', `export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>true});`);
  await host.install(manifest('provider', entry, [{ name: 'base.value', version: '1.0.0' }]), { enabled: true });
  await host.install(manifest('dependent', entry, [{ name: 'derived.value', version: '1.0.0' }], { requires: [{ name: 'base.value', version: '1.x' }] }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start(); let updates = 0; host.onCapabilitiesChanged(() => updates++);
  const management = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'management' });
  await management.disable('provider'); await management.close();
  const deadline = Date.now() + 5000;
  while (host.capabilities().length && Date.now() < deadline) await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
  assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []); assert.ok(updates > 0);
  assert.equal(host.list().find(p => p.manifest.id === 'dependent').state, 'faulted');
});

test('capability version ranges do not silently cross incompatible zero-major versions', () => {
  assert.equal(compatible('1.9.0', '^1.2.0'), true);
  assert.equal(compatible('2.0.0', '^1.2.0'), false);
  assert.equal(compatible('0.3.0', '^0.2.1'), false);
  assert.equal(compatible('0.2.9', '^0.2.1'), true);
  assert.equal(compatible('0.0.2', '^0.0.1'), false);
  assert.equal(compatible('1.5.0', '1.x'), true);
});

test('unproven process termination blocks host closure and replacement until resource release succeeds', { timeout: 15000 }, async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('retained-process', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>true});");
  await host.install(manifest('retained-process', entry, undefined, { isolation: 'process', trusted: false }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start();
  // Simulate an OS refusing termination after the active advertisement is removed.
  // Public close/enable must still retain ownership of that exact live driver.
  const driver = host.drivers.get('retained-process'), originalStop = driver.stop.bind(driver);
  let attempts = 0;
  driver.stop = async () => { attempts++; throw Object.assign(new Error('fixture OS termination refusal'), { code: 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED' }); };
  try {
    await assert.rejects(host.disable('retained-process'), { code: 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED' });
    assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []);
    await assert.rejects(host.enable('retained-process'), { code: 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED' });
    await assert.rejects(host.close(), { code: 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED' });
    assert.ok(attempts >= 3, 'Retired process ownership was forgotten');
  } finally {
    driver.stop = originalStop;
    await host.close();
  }
});

test('a cooperative cleanup rejection retains its instance and is retried before closure succeeds', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('cooperative-cleanup', "import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';export const createPlugin=c=>{let attempts=0;return {invoke:()=>true,async stop(){if(++attempts===1)throw new Error('fixture cleanup refused');await fs.writeFile(path.join(c.dataRoot,'cleanup-complete'),String(attempts))}}};");
  await host.install(manifest('cooperative-cleanup', entry), { enabled: true }); await host.start();
  const concurrent = await Promise.allSettled([host.close(), host.close()]);
  for (const result of concurrent) { assert.equal(result.status, 'rejected'); assert.equal(result.reason.code, 'PLUGIN_TERMINATION_FAILED'); }
  assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []);
  await assert.rejects(fs.stat(path.join(host.dataRoot('cooperative-cleanup'), 'cleanup-complete')), { code: 'ENOENT' });
  await host.close();
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(host.dataRoot('cooperative-cleanup'), 'cleanup-complete'), 'utf8'), '2');
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { PluginHost } from '../../dist/platform/plugin-host.js';
import { createPluginServices } from '../../dist/platform/plugin-services.js';

async function fixture(t) {
  const root = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-v12-services-'));
  const host = new PluginHost({ stateRoot: root, deviceId: 'service-fixture' });
  t.after(async () => { await host.close(); await fs.rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true, maxRetries: 5, retryDelay: 50 }); });
  const write = async (name, text, extension = 'mjs') => {
    const entry = path.join(root, name + '.' + extension); await fs.writeFile(entry, text); return entry;
  };
  const manifest = (id, entry, capability, extra = {}) => ({
    id, entry, version: '1.0.0', dataSchemaVersion: 1, isolation: 'in-process', trusted: true,
    capabilities: [{ name: capability, version: '1.2.0' }], timeoutMs: 2000, ...extra
  });
  return { root, host, write, manifest };
}
async function until(check, message) {
  const deadline = Date.now() + 4000;
  while (Date.now() < deadline) { if (await check()) return; await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 20)); }
  assert.fail(message);
}

test('service binding restricts declarations and versions, carries cancellation, and closes with its parent scope', async () => {
  const controller = new AbortController(), calls = [];
  const requirements = [{ name: 'math.scale', version: '^1.2.0' }];
  const services = createPluginServices(requirements, async (name, input, options) => {
    calls.push({ name, input, options }); return Number(input) * 3;
  }, controller.signal);
  requirements[0].version = '*';
  assert.equal(await services.invoke('math.scale', 7), 21);
  assert.equal(calls[0].options.version, '^1.2.0');
  assert.equal(calls[0].options.compose, false);
  assert.equal(calls[0].options.signal, controller.signal);
  await assert.rejects(services.invoke('private.read', {}), { code: 'CAPABILITY_DEPENDENCY_UNDECLARED' });
  assert.equal(calls.length, 1);
  controller.abort();
  await assert.rejects(services.invoke('math.scale', 9), { code: 'CANCELLED' });
  assert.equal(calls.length, 1);
});

for (const isolation of ['in-process', 'process']) {
  test(isolation + ' dependent really invokes its version-compatible provider during init and invocation', { timeout: 15000 }, async t => {
    const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
    const old = await write('old', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>({wrongVersion:true})});");
    const provider = await write('provider', "export const createPlugin=()=>{let count=0;return {invoke(n,i){return {scaled:Number(i)*3,count:++count}}}};");
    const dependent = await write('dependent', "export async function createPlugin(c){const initial=await c.services.invoke('math.scale',2);return {async invoke(n,i,ctx){if(i==='expired')return c.services.invoke('math.scale',9);return {initial,live:await ctx.services.invoke('math.scale',i)}}}}");
    await host.install(manifest('old', old, 'math.scale', { capabilities: [{ name: 'math.scale', version: '1.0.0', priority: 100 }] }), { enabled: true });
    await host.install(manifest('provider', provider, 'math.scale'), { enabled: true });
    await host.install(manifest('dependent', dependent, 'math.derived', {
      isolation, trusted: isolation === 'in-process', requires: [{ name: 'math.scale', version: '^1.2.0' }]
    }), { enabled: true });
    await host.start();
    assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('math.derived', 7), { initial: { scaled: 6, count: 1 }, live: { scaled: 21, count: 2 } });
    await assert.rejects(host.invoke('math.derived', 'expired'), { code: 'CANCELLED' });
    assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('math.derived', 8), { initial: { scaled: 6, count: 1 }, live: { scaled: 24, count: 3 } });
  });
}

test('an augmenter can call its declared provider without composing itself recursively', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const base = await write('base', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:(n,i)=>({value:i})});");
  const augment = await write('augment', "export const createPlugin=()=>({async invoke(n,{input,result},ctx){return {...result,derived:await ctx.services.invoke('text.base',input+'!')}}});");

  await host.install(manifest('augment', augment, 'text.base', {
    capabilities: [{ name: 'text.base', version: '1.2.0', role: 'augmenter' }], requires: [{ name: 'text.base', version: '^1.0.0' }]
  }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start();
  assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('text.base', 'hello'), { value: 'hello', derived: { value: 'hello!' } });
});

test('undeclared services are denied before a real provider receives a call', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const provider = await write('secret', "export const createPlugin=()=>{let count=0;return {invoke(){return ++count}}};");
  const dependent = await write('consumer', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:(n,i,c)=>c.services.invoke('private.value',i)});");

  await host.install(manifest('consumer', dependent, 'derived.value', { isolation: 'process', trusted: false }), { enabled: true });

  assert.ok(host.capabilities().includes('derived.value'));
});

test('a non-Node JSON-lines sidecar invokes dependencies and callback ids cannot settle ordinary requests', { timeout: 15000 }, async t => {
  let executable=process.env.DEVICE_PARITY_PYTHON;
  if(!executable&&process.platform==='win32'){
    const resolved=spawnSync('py',['-3','-c','import sys;print(sys.executable)'],{encoding:'utf8',windowsHide:true});
    if(resolved.status===0)executable=resolved.stdout.trim();
  }
  executable??=process.platform==='win32'?'python':'python3';
  if (spawnSync(executable, ['--version'], { windowsHide: true }).status !== 0) { t.skip('Python executable unavailable'); return; }
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const provider = await write('scale', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:(n,i)=>({scaled:i*5})});");
  const python = await write('external', [
    'import sys,json',
    'pending={}',
    'def send(value):',
    ' print(json.dumps(value),flush=True)',
    'for line in sys.stdin:',
    ' m=json.loads(line)',
    ' if m.get("type")=="host_result":',
    '  parent=pending.pop(m["id"],None)',
    '  if parent:',
    '   if "error" in m: send({"id":parent,"error":m["error"]})',
    '   else: send({"id":parent,"result":{"transport":"python","provider":m.get("result")}})',
    ' elif m.get("method")=="init": send({"id":m["id"],"result":{"ready":True}})',
    ' elif m.get("method")=="invoke":',
    '  pending[m["id"]]=m["id"]',
    '  value=m["params"]["input"]',
    '  send({"type":"host_call","id":m["id"],"parent_id":"expired" if value=="expired" else m["id"],"method":"capability.invoke","params":{"name":"math.external","input":value}})',
    ' elif m.get("method")=="stop":',
    '  send({"id":m["id"],"result":{"stopped":True}})',
    '  break',
    ''
  ].join('\n'), 'py');
  await host.install(manifest('scale', provider, 'math.external'), { enabled: true });
  await host.install(manifest('external', python, 'derived.external', {
    isolation: 'process', trusted: false, executable, args: [python], requires: [{ name: 'math.external', version: '1.x' }]
  }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start();
  assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('derived.external', 6), { transport: 'python', provider: { scaled: 30 } });
  await assert.rejects(host.invoke('derived.external', 'expired'), { code: 'CAPABILITY_PARENT_EXPIRED' });
  assert.deepEqual(await host.invoke('derived.external', 9), { transport: 'python', provider: { scaled: 45 } });
});

for (const trigger of ['cancel', 'deadline', 'disable']) {
  test('process dependency work is cancelled on parent ' + trigger, { timeout: 15000 }, async t => {
    const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
    const provider = await write('waiting', "export const createPlugin=()=>{let started=0,aborted=0;return {invoke(n,i,c){if(i==='status')return {started,aborted};started++;return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const timer=setTimeout(()=>resolve('late'),8000);c.signal.addEventListener('abort',()=>{aborted++;clearTimeout(timer);reject(Object.assign(new Error('cancelled'),{code:'CANCELLED'}))},{once:true})})}}};");
    const dependent = await write('waiter', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:(n,i,c)=>c.services.invoke('wait.base',i)});");
    await host.install(manifest('waiting', provider, 'wait.base', { timeoutMs: 10000 }), { enabled: true });
    await host.install(manifest('waiter', dependent, 'wait.derived', {
      isolation: 'process', trusted: false, timeoutMs: trigger === 'deadline' ? 1200 : 4000,
      requires: [{ name: 'wait.base', version: '^1.0.0' }]
    }), { enabled: true });
    await host.start();
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const count = trigger === 'disable' ? 4 : 1;
    const outcomes = Array.from({ length: count }, () => host.invoke('wait.derived', 'wait', { signal: controller.signal })
      .then(value => ({ value }), error => ({ error })));
    await until(async () => (await host.invoke('wait.base', 'status')).started === count, 'Provider never started all requests');
    if (trigger === 'cancel') controller.abort();
    if (trigger === 'disable') await host.disable('waiter');
    for (const completed of await Promise.all(outcomes)) {
      assert.ok(completed.error, 'Cancelled dependency unexpectedly succeeded');
      assert.equal(completed.error.code, trigger === 'deadline' ? 'PLUGIN_TIMEOUT' : trigger === 'disable' ? 'PLUGIN_STOPPED' : 'CANCELLED');
    }
    await until(async () => (await host.invoke('wait.base', 'status')).aborted === count, 'Provider did not receive all aborts');
    assert.ok(host.capabilities().includes('wait.base'));
  });
}

test('loss of a pinned provider cannot turn a formerly acyclic dependency graph into fallback recursion', async t => {
  const { host, write, manifest } = await fixture(t);
  const entry = await write('graph', "export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>true});");
  await host.install(manifest('root-provider', entry, 'graph.x', { capabilities: [{ name: 'graph.x', version: '1.2.0', priority: 100 }] }), { enabled: true });
  await host.install(manifest('middle', entry, 'graph.y', { requires: [{ name: 'graph.x', version: '1.x' }] }), { enabled: true });
  await host.install(manifest('leaf', entry, 'graph.x', { requires: [{ name: 'graph.y', version: '1.x' }] }), { enabled: true });
  await host.start();
  assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), ['graph.x', 'graph.y']);
  await host.disable('root-provider');
  assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []);
  assert.equal(host.list().find(item => item.manifest.id === 'middle').state, 'faulted');
  assert.equal(host.list().find(item => item.manifest.id === 'leaf').state, 'faulted');
  // The only fallback is itself faulted after losing its pinned provider.
  // Sticky fault policy excludes it before graph traversal: retrying middle
  // must not implicitly resurrect leaf in order to discover the old cycle.
  await assert.rejects(host.enable('middle'), { code: 'CAPABILITY_DEPENDENCY_MISSING' });
  assert.equal(host.list().find(item => item.manifest.id === 'leaf').state, 'faulted');
  assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), []);
  await host.enable('root-provider');
  assert.equal(host.list().find(item => item.manifest.id === 'middle').state, 'faulted');
  assert.equal(host.list().find(item => item.manifest.id === 'leaf').state, 'faulted');
  await host.enable('middle');
  await host.enable('leaf');
  assert.deepEqual(host.capabilities(), ['graph.x', 'graph.y']);
});

===== BEGIN SOURCE test/v12/server-broker.test.mjs | original-bytes=10171 | sha256=02a077841fb9c3798142c0551ab7c383b441a5963297632e6e8a92cd37c4958c =====
import test, { after } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { Server as NetServer } from 'node:net';
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { createHash, generateKeyPairSync, randomBytes, sign } from 'node:crypto';

import { buildDeviceAuthChallenge, createDeviceBroker } from '../../control-plane/scripts/device-broker.mjs';
import { createDeviceStore } from '../../control-plane/scripts/device-store.mjs';
import { createDevicePairingStore } from '../../control-plane/scripts/device-pairing-store.mjs';
import { createAccountStore } from '../../control-plane/scripts/account-store.mjs';

// This slice is deliberately incapable of opening any port, including port 0.
const originalListen = NetServer.prototype.listen;
NetServer.prototype.listen = function () {
  throw new Error('The embedded broker must not create a network listener.');
};
after(() => { NetServer.prototype.listen = originalListen; });

function keyPair() {
  const pair = generateKeyPairSync('ed25519');
  return { privateKey: pair.privateKey, publicKeyPem: pair.publicKey.export({ type: 'spki', format: 'pem' }).toString() };
}
function signer(deviceId, keys) {
  return nonce => sign(null, buildDeviceAuthChallenge({ deviceId, nonce }), keys.privateKey).toString('base64');
}
function deferred() {
  let resolve;
  const promise = new Promise(done => { resolve = done; });
  return { promise, resolve };
}
function fixture(t) {
  const stateRoot = mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'mcp-v12-broker-'));
  const dbPath = path.join(stateRoot, 'gateway.sqlite');
  const accounts = createAccountStore({ dbPath });
  const alice = accounts.createAccount({ email: 'alice@example.test', password: 'fixture-password-alice' });
  const bob = accounts.createAccount({ email: 'bob@example.test', password: 'fixture-password-bob' });
  const devices = createDeviceStore({ dbPath });
  const pairings = createDevicePairingStore({ dbPath });
  const broker = createDeviceBroker({ deviceStore: devices, pairingStore: pairings, requestTimeoutMs: 1000, helloTimeoutMs: 1000 });
  t.after(async () => {
    await broker.shutdown();
    pairings.close(); devices.close(); accounts.close();
    rmSync(stateRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });
  const deviceId = 'embedded-fixture';
  const keys = keyPair();
  function grant(owner = alice) {
    const verifier = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
    const started = pairings.start({
      clientId: 'embedded-fixture-client', deviceId, deviceName: 'Fixture Device',
      publicKeyPem: keys.publicKeyPem,
      codeChallenge: createHash('sha256').update(verifier).digest('base64url')
    });
    assert.equal(pairings.poll({ deviceCode: started.deviceCode, clientId: 'embedded-fixture-client', codeVerifier: verifier }).status, 'authorization_pending');
    assert.throws(() => pairings.poll({ deviceCode: started.deviceCode, clientId: 'embedded-fixture-client', codeVerifier: 'wrong-verifier' }), /PKCE/);
    pairings.approve({ userCode: started.userCode, accountId: owner.accountId, accountLabel: owner.email });
    return pairings.poll({ deviceCode: started.deviceCode, clientId: 'embedded-fixture-client', codeVerifier: verifier }).enrollmentGrant;
  }
  function connect(overrides = {}) {
    return broker.attachLocalDevice({
      deviceId, publicKeyPem: keys.publicKeyPem, signChallenge: signer(deviceId, keys),
      invoke: async (_tool, args) => ({ echo: args }), capabilities: ['read_file'],
      executionRuntimeGeneration: 'same-core-runtime', runtimeReady: true,
      agentVersion: 'mcp-device/1.0.12', packageVersion: '1.0.12', ...overrides
    });
  }
  const call = (overrides = {}) => broker.callDevice({
    accountId: alice.accountId, deviceId, tool: 'read_file', arguments: { path: 'fixture.txt' }, ...overrides
  });
  return { accounts, alice, bob, devices, pairings, broker, deviceId, keys, grant, connect, call };
}

test('embedded claim uses ordinary PKCE + signed grant; ownerless and cross-account calls never reach Core', async t => {
  const f = fixture(t);
  let invoked = 0;
  await assert.rejects(f.connect(), /unknown|revoked|auth/i);
  assert.equal(f.devices.get(f.deviceId), null);
  await f.connect({ enrollmentGrant: f.grant(), invoke: async (tool, args) => { invoked += 1; return { tool, args, core: 'same-instance' }; } });
  assert.equal(f.devices.get(f.deviceId).ownerAccountId, f.alice.accountId);
  assert.deepEqual(f.broker.listDevices(), []);
  assert.deepEqual(f.broker.listDevices({ accountId: f.bob.accountId }), []);
  await assert.rejects(f.call({ accountId: null }), /ACCOUNT_ID_REQUIRED/);
  await assert.rejects(f.call({ accountId: f.bob.accountId }), /DEVICE_ACCESS_DENIED/);
  assert.equal(invoked, 0);
  const result = await f.call({ includeDispatchContext: true });
  assert.deepEqual(result.result, { tool: 'read_file', args: { path: 'fixture.txt' }, core: 'same-instance' });
  assert.equal(result.dispatchContext.executionRuntimeGeneration, 'same-core-runtime');
  assert.equal(invoked, 1);
});

test('invalid proof cannot consume enrollment grant; a valid retry can claim exactly once', async t => {
  const f = fixture(t);
  const enrollmentGrant = f.grant();
  await assert.rejects(f.connect({ enrollmentGrant, signChallenge: signer(f.deviceId, keyPair()) }), /signature|auth/i);
  assert.equal(f.devices.get(f.deviceId), null);
  const endpoint = await f.connect({ enrollmentGrant });
  assert.equal(endpoint.isOpen(), true);
  assert.throws(() => f.pairings.consumeGrant({ enrollmentGrant, deviceId: f.deviceId, publicKeyPem: f.keys.publicKeyPem }), /consumed/);
});

test('replacing embedded endpoint rejects old pending result without replay and preserves process affinity checks', async t => {
  const f = fixture(t);
  const started = deferred(); const completion = deferred();
  let firstInvocations = 0; let replacementInvocations = 0;
  const first = await f.connect({ enrollmentGrant: f.grant(), invoke: async () => { firstInvocations += 1; started.resolve(); return completion.promise; } });
  const pending = f.call();
  const rejection = assert.rejects(pending, /replaced.*not replayed/i);
  await started.promise;
  const replacement = await f.connect({ invoke: async () => { replacementInvocations += 1; return { fresh: true }; } });
  await rejection;
  completion.resolve({ stale: true });
  await new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
  assert.equal(first.isOpen(), false);
  assert.ok(replacement.connectionEpoch > first.connectionEpoch);
  await assert.rejects(f.call({ expectedConnectionEpoch: first.connectionEpoch }), error => error.code === 'PROCESS_SESSION_STALE');
  await assert.rejects(f.call({ expectedExecutionRuntimeGeneration: 'old-runtime' }), error => error.code === 'PROCESS_SESSION_STALE');
  assert.equal(replacementInvocations, 0);
  assert.deepEqual(await f.call(), { fresh: true });
  assert.equal(firstInvocations, 1);
  assert.equal(replacementInvocations, 1);
});

test('rotation ABA invalidates pending embedded result by authorization generation', async t => {
  const f = fixture(t);
  const started = deferred(); const completion = deferred();
  await f.connect({ enrollmentGrant: f.grant(), invoke: async () => { started.resolve(); return completion.promise; } });
  const pending = f.call();
  const rejection = assert.rejects(pending, /authorization|disconnected|offline/i);
  await started.promise;
  const next = keyPair();
  f.devices.rotate({ deviceId: f.deviceId, expectedPublicKeyPem: f.keys.publicKeyPem, publicKeyPem: next.publicKeyPem });
  f.devices.rotate({ deviceId: f.deviceId, expectedPublicKeyPem: next.publicKeyPem, publicKeyPem: f.keys.publicKeyPem });
  completion.resolve({ mustNotBeAccepted: true });
  await rejection;
  assert.equal(f.broker.listDevices({ accountId: f.alice.accountId })[0].online, false);
  await assert.rejects(f.call(), error => error.code === 'DEVICE_OFFLINE');
  await f.connect();
  assert.deepEqual(await f.call(), { echo: { path: 'fixture.txt' } });
});

test('capability updates use broker authorization checks; revoked embedded endpoints cannot dispatch', async t => {
  const f = fixture(t);
  let invoked = 0;
  const endpoint = await f.connect({
    enrollmentGrant: f.grant(), capabilities: ['read_file', 'knowledge.search'],
    invoke: async () => { invoked += 1; return { ok: true }; }
  });
  endpoint.updateCapabilities(['read_file']);
  assert.deepEqual(f.broker.listDevices({ accountId: f.alice.accountId })[0].capabilities, ['read_file']);
  await assert.rejects(f.call({ tool: 'knowledge.search' }), /does not advertise/);
  assert.deepEqual(await f.call(), { ok: true });
  f.devices.revoke(f.deviceId);
  endpoint.updateCapabilities(['read_file', 'knowledge.search']);
  assert.equal(endpoint.isOpen(), false);
  await assert.rejects(f.call(), error => error.code === 'DEVICE_OFFLINE');
  assert.equal(invoked, 1);
});
